Don't transition that opacity on the store modal — its wrapper holds the backdrop too, so fading it animates the whole dim on close. `closeProductModal()` must close an open lightbox first: browser-Back closes the modal from under it, which otherwise stranded the lightbox and left the modal stuck at `opacity:0` (invisible on next open). **Modal frame + scroll-lock (2026-07-26):** both modals' image frame is a fixed **5:4** — cards are 1:1, but a full square was too tall to see the details below and landscape cut too much. A per-image **dynamic** aspect ratio was tried and reverted 2026-07-26 (it varied the modal height per product). Both modals lock background scroll on **`<html>` (`overflowY`), never `<body>`**: body `overflow` makes it a BFC + scroll container, opening a phantom top-gap AND re-binding the store banner's sticky so it jumps on open/close.
- **Categories (hierarchical):** per-store tree, max 3 levels (`store-categories.ts`); Settings gets a tree editor (`category-tree.ts`), product forms a nested dropdown-in-dropdown picker (`category-picker.ts`). **Gotcha:** `store-categories.ts` uses `node:fs` — never let client-bundled code value-import it even transitively (it once crashed the whole dashboard via `csv-bulk.ts`; now server-only). CSV uses 3 columns and treats a filled subcategory with an empty parent as a hard error. `Store.categories?:string[]` (flat tags for homepage browse shelves) is deliberately separate from the per-product tree. **Chips carry a subtree product count + a chevron when they drill down (2026-07-29)** — `countProductsPerCategory()` counts the whole subtree so the number always matches what clicking the chip filters to; shipped to the client as its own `#store-category-counts` JSON payload (not folded into the tree — the tree is persisted shape, the counts are derived) so all THREE client renderers (chip row, hover dropdown, mobile drawer) keep them after a drill-down. Zero renders as no badge, not "0".
- **Variants & inventory:** `StoreProduct.variants?`+`variantStock?`+`variantSku?` (`variant-combo.ts`). The dashboard combo grid has a sortable column per dimension + sticky Stock/Total. **`variantStock` is PARTIAL and the UI now says so (2026-08-02, owner-decided):** a blank combo cell means "no count of its own" and that combo sells from the shared pool with its siblings; top-level "מלאי" IS that pool and locks to the sum only once EVERY combo is counted. The even split that used to fill the grid from the pool (10 units, 4 combos → 3/3/2/2, saved as fact) is gone — a seller holding 8 red and 2 blue had the 6th red sale refused and three blue oversold. It lived in FOUR private copies (lib, dashboard.astro, products.ts, api) so deleting one changed nothing and every test stayed green; `tests/product-total-stock.test.ts` greps the source for a new one. Totals = counted buckets + the pool ONCE if any combo is still pooled (`tests/combo-total-rule.test.ts`). CSV carries a pooled combo as a BLANK cell both ways — export wrote the pool into each combo's own cell and re-import read them back as buckets, turning 10 units into 40 (`tests/csv-pooled-stock-roundtrip.test.ts`). The products-table per-variant stock dropdown is **inline-editable** (`products.ts#activateComboStockEdit` + a server render kept in sync); `/api/product` `patch-variant-stock` writes ONE combo's bucket and leaves the others pooled. Color recognition (`color-variants.ts`); an unrecognized color gets a native color-picker chip. A variant type name can't duplicate another, synonym-aware, enforced client+server. **`variantSku` is set only via CSV import** — the single-product editor preserves it but has no UI, and on an options edit `product.ts` filters it to still-valid comboKeys so a renamed combo can't inherit a stale code; the feed uses it as each variant row's `mpn`. **Checkout-time atomic enforcement:** `decrementStock()`/`restockProduct()` are one conditional `UPDATE` each (no mutex since 2026-08-02); `/api/checkout` rolls back everything already reserved if one item lacks stock (409). **And the selection is RESOLVED against the product first (2026-08-12, area-audit 3):** `resolveSelection()` refuses one the product does not declare, because "no bucket matched" is a legitimate state down there — it is how a pooled combo sells — so an invented selection was indistinguishable from it and, on a fully-counted product where the pool IS the sum of the buckets, bought against the total. Returns the DECLARED strings so whitespace cannot mint a second comboKey. Same rule on the read side: `/api/cart/prices` marks such a line `gone` instead of quoting the pool as its ceiling. `tests/variant-selection-guard.test.ts` scans the tree for both. **The seller's side of the same number — compare-and-set (2026-07-30):** stock is the one field a seller writes as an ABSOLUTE value that the SERVER also writes (every sale decrements it), so a figure typed over a cell that has since been sold down would resurrect the sold unit and let it be sold twice. The full edit form was already safe (`stock` ∈ `PRODUCT_REV_FIELDS` → `mergeByFieldRev` keeps the stored value for an untouched field and 409s when both sides changed); the two INLINE editors — products-table cell and the per-combo dropdown — deliberately skip that merge as "one explicit intent", so each now sends the number it DISPLAYED as `prevStock` and `/api/product` refuses with `409 {conflict:true, conflictFields:['stock'], currentStock}` if the stored value moved. The client answers by correcting the cell to the real number rather than letting the seller retype a stale one. No `prevStock` (older bundle) → the write proceeds, per the additive/zero-downtime rule. `tests/product-stock-cas.test.ts`. **Generalise before adding a field:** any absolute-value field the server also writes needs this, not a plain write — stock is the only one today.
- **Product search tags (סשן ד׳):** `tag-suggest.ts` — a product's `tags` feed site/product search (name+tags, NOT description). **Variant values are searchable at the SOURCE since 2026-08-14 (migration 0027)** — `search_text` reads `variants` directly, so a colour is findable however the product arrived; the auto-tag copy below still runs but search no longer depends on it (CSV bulk import never derived tags, and a derivation is a snapshot that goes stale). The editor shows click-to-add suggestion chips from a curated lexicon; category-path segments + variant values are auto-applied on save (`withAutoTags`; on edit only NEW sources are derived, so a removed auto-tag stays removed). Distinct from ad `custom_labels`. AI extraction was explored + removed — the real engine is deferred to DB semantic search (memory `project_ai_tagging_deferred`).
- **i18n / SEO / AIO:** `he`/`en` dictionaries, `getLang`/`getDir`/`getT`, lang cookie, RTL throughout; `Seo.astro`, JSON-LD, skip link, focus traps, ARIA. **Sitemap = two surfaces (2026-07-20):** static (`@astrojs/sitemap`, build-time public routes, private ones removed via config `filter`) + dynamic `/sitemap-content.xml` enumerating every visible SSR store/product page — they're `prerender=false` and therefore absent from the static one, the silent gap that once left all products un-indexed. Both are listed in `robots.txt`, which must keep `/api/feed/` crawlable for Merchant Center. `trailingSlash:'never'` + a query-stripped, slash-free canonical consolidates filtered/sorted views. The store page passes its banner as the OG image. **AIO (2026-07-21):** enriched Product JSON-LD (`brand`/`sku`/`mpn`/`itemCondition`), a dynamic `/llms.txt` (config-derived → the real domain auto-propagates), `robots.txt` opened to AI crawlers (GPTBot/OAI-SearchBot/PerplexityBot/ClaudeBot/Google-Extended…), and **IndexNow** (`indexnow.ts`, pinged on EVERY mutation that changes a public page — create/edit/inline-edit/images/bulk-discount/delete/visibility/CSV import/store save; only a stock-only write is deliberately silent, since checkout decrements stock and the feed's `availability` self-corrects. Guarded by a branch-list test in `tests/indexnow.test.ts`, because the 2026-08-05 bug was edit+delete never pinging while this line claimed they did) pushing URLs to Bing → ChatGPT/Copilot — a no-op until `seo.indexNowKey` is set on a real (non-example.com) domain; verification file at `/{key}.txt`. Post-launch owner steps: GO_LIVE §7. **The outbound seam, guarded (2026-08-04):** every surface that hands data to a system that is not ours — feed, dataLayer/fbq, sitemap, canonical, llms.txt, IndexNow, a `Location` header — now has one executable contract, `tests/external-contract.test.ts`. It found four live defects the day it was written, all invisible from every screen: a Hebrew redirect THREW instead of redirecting (`Astro.params` is decoded; a header is a byte string) so the slug-rename 301s and the custom-domain 301 were 500s; the feed's `<link>` ignored a verified custom domain and so submitted a cross-domain redirect; every Hebrew variant product broke Google's 50-char `id` cap, and since a variant product emits no parent row it was absent from the catalogue entirely; and no attribute obeyed its published limit. The mechanism, not the fixes, is the point: the test runs an adversarial Hebrew product through the real builder, reads the tag list OFF THE SERIALIZER so a fixture cannot hide an optional attribute, and FAILS ON AN ATTRIBUTE WHOSE LIMIT NOBODY DECLARED. Google's table is enforced for both networks because on every shared attribute Google's limit is the tighter one (checked against both specs); the one thing a local test cannot settle — Meta spells `availability` with a space — is logged with its trigger in GO_LIVE §2.5. **On-site semantic/contextual search is the crown-jewel next phase — DB-dependent, full strategy in `CONTEXTUAL_SEARCH_STRATEGY.md`.** **Lang is cookie-based on one URL → no hreflang; only the default (he) is indexable — architectural, revisit if EN SEO matters.** **Tracking:** `tracking.ts` → dataLayer + fbq; sellers never touch ad config. **HTML-injection defence — settled 2026-07-29, don't re-derive it per site.** Astro's SSR `{expr}` auto-escapes; the exposed surface is the ~36 files that build HTML as client-side template strings. Three sink classes, each now with ONE helper and a guard test that fails on the pattern: (1) **text/attributes** → `lib/html-escape.ts#escapeHtml` (escapes `&<>"'`; 20 local copies were folded in, call sites keep their old name via an import alias) — the quote was the recurring miss, because a value lands in `attr="…"` as often as in a text node; (2) **data inside `<script>`** → `lib/json-script.ts#jsonForScript` — `JSON.stringify` is a sink, `</script>` is valid JSON and closes the tag (11 of 15 sinks were raw, incl. the JSON-LD on every public page and `BaseLayout`'s dataLayer push, which lands in executable JS and so also needs U+2028/2029 escaped); (3) **image URLs at the entry point** → `lib/image-url.ts` (https or site-relative only, stored as the URL parser's re-serialization so quotes come back percent-encoded). Escaping at render is the last line, not the only one. **Merchant listings + seller-facing SEO (2026-08-04):** the Offer now carries `shippingDetails` (derived from `SHIPPING_RATES`, so it can never disagree with what checkout charges — `deliveryTime` deliberately absent until a carrier gives real numbers) and `priceValidUntil` (from the WINNING discount only, the day AFTER the inclusive `endsAt`); the homepage's JSON-LD became an `@graph` of Organization + **WebSite/SearchAction** (sitelinks search box, and the machine-readable statement that the catalog IS queryable). `hasMerchantReturnPolicy` is the one piece still missing and it is BLOCKED ON A POLICY DECISION, not on code — GO_LIVE §5. **`StoreProduct.brand?`** — the manufacturer's brand for a reseller, empty falling back to the store name exactly as the feed and JSON-LD always did (Merchant Center matches on brand, so a distributor's product wearing the shop's name never joins the real listing). **The seller now gets told what to write:** `product-seo-hints.ts` derives per-product advice (state-free, like the onboarding checklist) and `product-seo-field.ts` renders it plus a live search-result preview into BOTH product forms. It gates nothing — no overlay, no link, nothing disabled — because a seller must never be thrown out of a half-filled form. Only the missing image is `required`, and only because the ad feed drops such a product outright. **The panel leads with a fill bar (2026-08-10, `productSeoScore`/`productSeoMeterView`):** a bare "1 מתוך 5" beside the preview label was read once and skipped. Three bands — and their boundaries are `needsSeoAttention` itself, not new thresholds, so the meter and the products-table marker can never disagree about one product (pinned by an invariant in the test). No red band: a half-filled form is a form being filled, and amber (`weak`) is the only state with a real consequence behind it. The bar lives in the panel SHELL, not in `data-seo-body` which the script replaces on every keystroke — a fresh element has no previous width to transition from, so it would jump instead of fill; the script writes four values onto the persistent element, all from the one function that also rendered the markup server-side. **The LIST shows the same verdict (2026-08-12, `productSeoRowGaugeHtml`):** one dial per row, all three bands, in its own narrow column immediately before the actions button, plus a `pseo` band filter whose trigger IS that column's heading — a word plus a separate funnel needs ~48px against a 21px dial, and then whichever is aligned to the column leaves the other visibly offset. A COLUMN and not a badge beside the name (tried 2026-08-04, removed: a text pill wrapped and read as a fault) and not overlaid on the thumbnail, whose position moves with the row's own height. Weak-only was the first rule and it was wrong here: density reads as decoration beside a name, but a column of dials sorts a catalogue at a glance and the two good bands are the half a seller wants. Two traps only measurement finds — `reset.css`'s `svg{max-width:100%}` resolves against the mobile thumbnail cell, which collapses to ZERO width for an image-less product; and `.products-table td/th` sets cell padding INSIDE each breakpoint block, above the reach of a base rule, so an alignment change measured as no change at all. **Auth storage, same audit:** scrypt (was HMAC-SHA256 — fast, so a leaked `sellers.json` brute-forces at GPU speed), `timingSafeEqual` compares, legacy records upgraded on next login; `AUTH_SECRET` throws at boot in prod instead of falling back to the repo's public dev default (GO_LIVE §7).
- **Operational error logging:** `middleware.ts` logs to the `error_log` table (capped, one statement; the old `data/error-log.json` went with the DB migration) only on a genuinely thrown/uncaught exception — deliberately **not** any route returning `>=500` (an intentional 503 would be a content-free false positive). Client-side `error-reporter.ts` posts to the unauthenticated `/api/log-client-error` into the same log; its `route` field is spoofable client input, accepted as low-impact since it's reporting-only. **Identity enrichment:** `ErrorLogEntry` carries `storeSlug`/`actorRole`/`actorLabel`/`resolutionHint`; `resolveErrorContext()` wraps its own body in try/catch — it **must never throw**, since it runs inside the caller's own catch block, before `logError`'s. **Settings-save error tiers (סשן ב׳):** offline (fetch rejects) → "no connection" toast, no report (the server is unreachable, nothing to log to); 4xx-with-message → show the validation message, no report; unexpected (5xx/unparseable) → toast + best-effort `reportClientError()`. Genuine server throws already log via middleware, so the client only reports the gaps it can't see — no double-log. **Manual triage (2026-07-16):** `ErrorLogEntry.resolved?` — admin toggles a row "handled" (reversible, optimistic). Alerts is a real `<table>` (`table-layout:fixed`+`<colgroup>`) down to 640px, then a stacked card. Gotchas: an icon-only button needs its own explicit `flex-shrink:0` even if siblings have it; a per-column `<th>` `text-align` override needs `.class`+type-selector specificity to beat `.admin-table th{text-align:start}`.
- **Graceful degradation — audited as one surface (2026-08-06, `review-diff` area row 8).** The rule: a secondary service (analytics, feeds, notifications, background jobs) never sits in the pipeline of a browse, a checkout or a seller action. Four defects, each only reachable during a THIRD PARTY's outage and so invisible to every passing test: a throwing GTM tag stranded the buyer with a filled cart and no route to checkout (guard is `tap()` inside `lib/tracking.ts`, not the four call sites); the public feed + `sitemap-content.xml` rebuilt the whole catalogue per request on one event loop, so any concurrent pull stalled the mall (`lib/single-flight.ts` — a de-duplicator, deliberately NOT a cache); four bare BROWSER fetches of a stored Cloudinary original spun the seller's button forever (the server guard test cannot see that shape — the rule is in `outbound-fetch.ts`'s header); and a job that HANGS rather than throws stopped forever while `job_runs` kept the previous run's `'ok'` (every run is now abandoned at its own `leaseSec`). Also hardened: `adoptHandoff` cannot 500 a page, and `notifyOrderStatusChanged` is caught so a failed announcement cannot skip the store closure behind it. `tests/secondary-service-isolation.test.ts` holds the rule — a NAMED-LIST scan on purpose, since a transitive one false-positives on a provider the page never constructs.
- **Postgres foundation (2026-08-02, stage 0+1 of `DB_MIGRATION_PLAN.md`):** hosting decided — **Neon, `eu-central-1`** (§2.1: cheapest at this scale, pgvector + pooler built in, and the app server must sit in the SAME region, because the latency that costs is app↔DB per query, not user↔DB once per request). `src/lib/db.ts` is the lazy pool; `migrations/0001_init.sql` is the whole schema with its indexes and constraints; `npm run db:migrate` applies it (the one-time `db:import` is deleted — see stage 3); `docker-compose.yml` runs Postgres locally. **Stage 2 is under way on `db-stage-2`** — every module listed above plus `orders`, messages/notifications, the view/analytics buckets and buyer state are queries now (so `DATABASE_URL` is REQUIRED and the boot gate refuses to start without it); **stage 3 done (2026-08-03, §8 has the numbers)**: `data/*.json` + the `db:import` entrypoint deleted, `EXPLAIN ANALYZE` and the 50-concurrent-buyer check run against Neon. **§3 done (2026-08-03, `db-stage-getall`)**: the seven `getAll*` are gone or scoped — every whole-platform number (GMV headline, revenue per store, product counts, open orders, per-period sales, the seller funnel, the live reconciliation, campaign/subscription totals) is a `SUM`/`GROUP BY` in `lib/order-reporting.ts` and friends, the admin Orders and Messages tabs page in SQL, and platform product search is an indexed query (migration 0006) instead of reading the catalogue on every keystroke. The rule: a `GROUP BY` written as a `for` loop belongs in the database, arithmetic takes its data as a parameter — and every pure function that left the render path stayed as the query's twin, pinned by `tests/reporting-invariants.test.ts` §7. `getAllStores`/`getAllSellers` stay on purpose as the admin ROSTER (§8 says what that costs and when it has to change). The money journal opens on a **30-day default window** (owner, 2026-08-03) — it was the last unbounded read on the page; a `?mev=` permalink widens back to the row it names, and the default is deliberately not a "clearable" filter. **The same sweep for ONE STORE's orders, 2026-08-11 (owner: "at least stop loading all of infinity every time")** — §3 scoped the platform-wide reads and left `getOrdersByStoreSlug`, which five surfaces used to render fifteen rows, a month's chart, or a 15-second poll; each asks a window / a page / a watermark now (`orders.ts`), with a parity test and a route scan behind it. **Stage 4a done (2026-08-03, `db-stage-4a-scheduler`):** the three jobs that had no caller now run on a timer — expired-checkout purge, the external-feed pull and the campaign health sweep. The timer is in-process, but WHICH instance runs a job is a durable claim in `job_runs` (migration 0007), so N instances still means one run per period and a restart does not re-run everything; §8 says why `pg_advisory_lock` and an external cron both lost. Every job is idempotent and that is asserted, not assumed. Measured against the real data and fixed here rather than discovered later: money is integer agorot, product slugs are unique per-store not globally (47 real collisions), every optional flag is `NOT NULL DEFAULT false`, `money_events.order_id` is text (232 of 233 rows predate uuids), the visitor arrays became rows, and variant stock decrements atomically via affected-row count — which is what lets `mutex.ts` go. `tests/db-import.test.ts` runs all of it on an in-process Postgres, so this is verified, not asserted. Two counter files were deliberately NOT imported and §5 says why: `store-favorite-counts.json` was dead data, and `wishlist-counts.json` was keyed by bare product slug so its numbers were wrong before they arrived. Both modules are gone; the numbers are `COUNT(*)` on `wishlist_items` / `favorite_stores` in `user-carts.ts`.

---

## Project structure
<!-- REFERENCE INDEX — do NOT read linearly at session start. grep for the path/module a task touches (lines are `path ← note`); regenerate live with `ls`/glob if unsure it's current. -->
```
src/i18n/translations.ts + index.ts ← he+en string dictionaries; getLang/getDir/getT
src/config/store.config.ts      ← platform config + formatPrice + cdnSrc + cdnSrcSet (retina width-descriptor srcset for seller photos). Deliberately has NO commissionPercent — commission comes from the seller's tier in lib/pricing.ts, and the field carries a comment saying so
src/layouts/BaseLayout.astro    ← page shell (GTM+Pixel injection). `storeOwnsThisPage` (`storeSlug && !sellerMode`) is the ONE answer to "is this page the store's or ours", read by the tab icon and by Seo.astro — a second spelling of it is how the favicon and the title come to disagree. `storeMode` is the LAYOUT and thirteen pages of ours ask for it; it is not that question
src/lib/i18n-island.ts          ← needsDashboardI18n(pathname) — which routes carry the 34.9KB `dashboard` slice of `#i18n-data`. Its own function because the rule was an inline `'/admin/'` PREFIX under `trailingSlash:'never'`, so `/admin` itself matched nothing and every client-built label on the admin dashboard fell back to English inside a Hebrew UI, silently, for weeks
src/components/Seo.astro        ← all meta/OG/JSON-LD. `storeOwned` (from BaseLayout) drops the ` | Dezabin` suffix and hands `og:site_name` to the store — a shopper-facing store page is the store's, in the tab, in the Google result and on the card they forward. Google's site name itself is a domain/subdomain property and cannot be set for a subdirectory, so on our path the name above the result stays ours whatever is declared; the store's own domain is the whole mechanism (see the Custom domains bullet). Guarded: `tests/store-owns-its-title.test.ts`
src/components/CsrfField.astro  ← the CSRF token as a hidden input, for the six forms the BROWSER really submits (logout, language, store creation, the three sign-ins). Server-rendered so sign-in still works with JS off; NOT added to AJAX forms, several of which post `new FormData(form)` verbatim. tests/csrf.test.ts: a POST form is either data-unsaved-guard (AJAX) or carries this
src/components/Footer.astro     ← the ONE site-wide footer, rendered by BaseLayout on every page
src/components/ToastContainer.astro ← global toast host (BaseLayout)
src/components/BrandLogo.astro  ← the platform wordmark, one component so it cannot drift between header/auth/hero. Draws ONE svg (mark + outlined letters) from src/lib/brand-lockup.ts — no font, no text, so no fallback face can reach it; the `dz-logo` class is a styling-free hook the header needs to nudge it and nothing near it
src/lib/brand-lockup.ts         ← GENERATED (npm run brand:wordmark), do not hand-edit. The mark's path, the outlined "ezabin", the stroke that stands in for a weight Chakra Petch does not have, both viewBoxes, the gradient's userSpaceOnUse coordinates (objectBoundingBox would give every letter its own ramp) and the tagline's size + margin. THE single source for every logo surface
scripts/generate-wordmark.mjs   ← `npm run brand:wordmark`. Rebuilds the lockup from the fonts in assets/brand-fonts/ with opentype.js: re-derives the D against Chakra Petch's own stem/width/ascender, outlines the letters, and writes brand-lockup.ts + favicon.svg + public/brand/*.svg. Its header records what is AUTHORED (the octagon's cut, the two optical ratios, the tracking, the thickening, the two bisections) versus derived. Serialises paths itself — opentype's toPathData emits literal NaN for some coordinates, which silently truncates a path at render
public/brand/*.svg              ← the standalone logo files: wordmark / mark / full lockup, each in gradient, solid dark and white. Self-contained (outlines, no font, no CSS, no network) so they can go straight into print or a deck
assets/brand-fonts/             ← the two TTFs the generator reads, plus the OFL. NOT shipped to browsers and NOT a webfont — they exist so the wordmark can be redrawn offline
src/components/Header.astro     ← nav; notifications bell (polls 30s); back-to-store pill; avatar dropdown = app menu. Shadow is state-driven, never constant (2026-08-01): border only at rest, --shadow-bar once scrolled, and off again whenever something opaque is pinned flush under it — a sticky bar, or the store page's pinned banner before the curtain rises past the header line. All three phases are argued in styles/components/header.css
src/components/CartDrawer.astro
src/components/WishlistDrawer.astro
src/components/AddToCartPreview.astro  ← listens for cart.ts's `cart:item-added` and shows a brief auto-dismissing preview (desktop corner card / mobile bottom bar); never opens CartDrawer itself — its buttons are links into the existing flows
src/components/ConfirmModal.astro      ← global <dialog>, event-driven (confirm:open); onConfirm may be async (dot-pulse busy-state until resolved). OK button is DANGER-red by default; `tone:'primary'` in the detail makes it the ordinary primary, for confirming a positive action (reopening a store) — red must keep meaning "this takes something away"
src/components/ProductQuickView.astro  ← global PQV + lightbox; pqv:open event (cart/checkout/dashboard)
src/components/HomeProductCard.astro   ← one product tile in the homepage spotlight carousel (lifted out of index.astro)
src/components/StoreProductModal.astro ← global rich store product modal + own lightbox; store-product-modal:open event; fetches /api/store-product by slug (store cards + product-page related cards). **History gotcha (2026-07-23):** opening pushState's the product URL, but ONLY when not already on it (a duplicate entry made Back land on the same URL). Since that entry is a same-document sibling, after the URL is REFRESHED into a real product page, Back is a phantom popstate that swaps the URL without loading — so the popstate handler force-reloads and calls markStoreScrollReturn() so the store's own scroll restore still fires. **Scroll-restoration ownership (2026-07-23):** the pushState ALSO sets `history.scrollRestoration='manual'` on that entry — Chrome fixes a reload's mode from the value stored *before* the reload, so BaseLayout's head script (new document) is too late alone and the refreshed product page opened at the store's offset; handed back to 'auto' when Back closes the modal. **"לדף המוצר המלא" navigates with `location.replace`** so the real document takes OVER the modal's own entry (same URL) — one Back still lands on the store, the modal stays up until the new page paints, and nothing flashes. The two rejected alternatives: `history.back()`-then-navigate (fires popstate → closes the modal → bare store page sits on screen for the whole navigation) and replaceState-to-the-store-URL (leaves the store in history twice)
src/components/MessageCompose.astro    ← buyer contact form
src/components/ShareMenu.astro         ← share this page — WhatsApp/Facebook/Telegram/email/copy + the OS sheet where one exists. Props: url (ALWAYS the canonical — the seller's own domain when there is one, never a filtered/paginated/ad-landing URL), title, lang, contentType, storeId, itemId?. Every network item is a server-rendered `<a href>` (no JS, middle-clickable, `rel=nofollow noopener noreferrer`); only copy + the OS sheet are buttons, and the OS one ships hidden until the script sees `navigator.share`. Brand glyphs are monochrome `currentColor` on purpose — green is `--color-sale`'s one job. Panel is z-45: the sticky filter toolbar under the store banner is z-40 (same trap as `.store-details__content`), the header is 50
src/components/StoreAvatar.astro       ← store profile-image circle — ALWAYS round, and a rounded-square "logo plate" was built and reverted the same day (2026-08-05; the file carries why — it reproduced the product-thumbnail recipe exactly, so the store's mark read as a stray product). Sized by the caller: 56px on a store card, small elsewhere. No-upload fallback = the initial on that store's OWN storeMark gradient (pass `slug`, or every store greys out the same); shared by banner/homepage/messages. `sampleColor` opts the picture in to client colour-reading — it adds `crossorigin`, which BREAKS the image outright on a host without CORS, so it is gated on `cdnIsSampleable()` and never a default
src/lib/cart.ts                 ← CartItem, localStorage, events, syncCartImages, getCartQty (stock-ceiling lookup), applyServerPrices (price + stock re-sync, flags clampedTo/soldOut), applyStockLimit (clamp to the live count a 409 reported)
src/lib/cart-prices.ts          ← re-prices a stored cart against the live catalog via /api/cart/prices (same resolution /api/checkout does), on moments of attention rather than a timer; returns WHICH lines moved and which way, so only a rise on a line being bought stops a purchase; fetchUnavailableLines() answers the same `gone` question for any OTHER list (the wishlist), so the two never disagree about what may be bought
src/lib/wishlist.ts             ← WishlistItem, localStorage; count badge sync (the server-side count is COUNT(*) in user-carts.ts)
src/lib/recent-stores.ts        ← recently-visited-store slugs in `sn_recent_stores` cookie (SSR-read); pure parse/merge helpers; account-synced via cart-sync
src/lib/badge-ticker.ts         ← updateBadge() — shared odometer-roll + pop for header cart/wishlist/notif badges
src/lib/share.ts                ← SHARE_CHANNELS + shareChannelUrl()/shareMessage() — the ONE place a share URL is built, because each network takes the message apart differently (Facebook: url only, it reads the page's OG tags; WhatsApp: one blob containing the url; Telegram: the two separately; mailto: subject+body) and a wrong split still opens, just missing the title or doubling the link. Input url is the canonical, already segment-encoded, so only the query layer is encoded here — double-encoding is CORRECT
src/lib/toast.ts                ← showToast()/showErrorToast() — the ONE way to raise a notice (wraps ToastContainer's `toast:show`); native alert/confirm/prompt are banned site-wide, confirmations go through ConfirmModal's `confirm:open`
src/lib/ripple.ts + burst.ts    ← spawnRipple(); spawnBurst() (radial dot burst) + spawnHeartBurst() (wishlist "like" — hearts fan out of the icon + icon pop, reduced-motion-aware); both fixed-position so they escape rounded overflow:hidden
src/lib/carousel-dots.ts        ← initLiquidDots(slides, dots, onSettle?) — shared "liquid" scroll-linked dot indicators (product-card grid + store modal + PQV); returns setActive(idx) for dot-click/variant-image jumps
src/lib/read-more.ts            ← initReadMore() (inline read-more/less)
src/lib/seller-auth.ts          ← Seller interface, register/login/session, Google OAuth
src/lib/last-store.ts           ← saveLastStore/getLastStore/clearLastStore (sessionStorage) — back-to-store pill state
src/lib/scroll-restore.ts       ← saveStoreScroll/applyStoreScrollQuery/restoreStoreScroll/markStoreScrollReturn — store page scroll+filter restore on back-nav, gated to real returns via `document.referrer`; the marker bypasses that gate for the quick-view refresh→Back forced reload, whose referrer can't be trusted. That same reload is the ONE case where the browser's own restore must be switched off (head script in `[slug].astro` keys off the marker): its remembered offset is stale (0) and lands after ours, animating the page away under `scroll-behavior:smooth`. `scrollToStable` jumps immediately then re-asserts per frame — at restore time the grid is short (images undecoded) and a single scrollTo gets clamped (297 for a saved 2500) — bailing on any user scroll
src/lib/stores.ts               ← Store interface (shipping:{selfPickup?}, pausedAt/closePendingAt/closedAt) + CRUD, getStoreById, isStoreVisible/isStoreDiscoverable/canStoreSell + getVisibleStores (all re-exported from store-status.ts — never test the flags directly); slug/domain helpers: normalizeSlug, renameStoreSlug+previousSlugs, getStoreBy{CustomDomain,PreviousSlug}, findStoreBySlugOrPrevious, isSlugTaken/isReservedSlug (matches the slug's SKELETON, so a lookalike spelling of a platform route is reserved too)/isCustomDomainTaken. **Postgres since 2026-08-02** — every reader/writer is async; previousSlugs is its own table, customDomain.hostname its own column, a delete is deleted_at. updateStore writes ONLY the keys the call carried (a present `undefined` clears, an absent key does not)
src/lib/custom-domain.ts        ← custom-domain contract + host classification (isPlatformHost/isUnclaimedCustomHost) + canonical/301-redirect/link/rewrite helpers + provider selection (Cloudflare / dev stub)
src/lib/custom-domain-cloudflare.ts ← Cloudflare-for-SaaS adapter (env-gated CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN+ZONE_ID). `checkStatus` answers 'unknown' when the API could not be REACHED — a swallowed error must never read as "not verified"
src/lib/custom-domain-verify.ts ← the ONE re-verification step, called by the seller's "check" button AND the hourly job: asks the provider, writes status+checkedAt (0014), notifies on a change. 'unknown' writes no status; a pass that would demote more than `demoteCeiling(n)` = max(3, n/4) demotes NONE and logs it — a wrong zone id answers 'pending' for every hostname at once. **The pending→active transition is also where the hostname is CLAIMED off its previous owner** (`claimCustomDomainHostname`, moved here from registration by the row-5 audit, 2026-08-16): that delete hits a DIFFERENT store's 301 memory, and it used to fire on any seller typing any string
src/lib/unclaimed-host.ts       ← where a request on an external host NO store claims today goes: the migration-0015 previous-domain 301, the www/apex twin 301, and (row-5 audit, 2026-08-16) the two AT ONCE — the twin spelling of a hostname a store has moved off, which 404'd. Answers with the store's canonical directly rather than chaining through the other spelling. Lives out of middleware.ts so `tests/unclaimed-host-db.test.ts` can drive it against real rows: every rule here is a claim about ROWS, not about request plumbing
src/lib/custom-domain-links.ts  ← client: strip redundant /slug prefix from in-store links on the custom host (cosmetic), AND send every platform-owned link to the platform origin carrying the cart + identity (load-bearing). initAdLandingLinks() is the crossing that goes the OTHER way (2026-08-06): the ?ad=1 exemption is per-REQUEST, so it covered the landing and not the click after it — which 301'd onto the seller's origin and dropped sn_attr, i.e. the campaign that paid for the visit got no credit. Not fixable by putting a handoff on that 301: it is a 301, cached permanently, and a per-visitor token in a shared cache is a worse bug. **The cart fragment is rebuilt at CLICK time, not at rewrite time (refreshCheckoutCartFragment, 2026-08-10)** — a static checkout link is rewritten once, at load, so the two buttons that are REVEALED by add-to-cart always carried the empty basket the shopper landed with: add → pay → an empty checkout, no error anywhere. A re-scan cannot fix it (cross() only touches root-relative hrefs), so a delegated capture-phase listener re-stamps instead
src/lib/platform-routes.ts     ← import-free, shared server+client: which paths are the platform's whatever host they arrive on, and which belong to the serving host. Carries the origin-boundary decision
src/lib/cart-handoff.ts        ← encode/decode one store's cart for the URL fragment; everything read back is untrusted and bounded
src/lib/cross-origin-handoff.ts ← signed, short-lived visitor-id + ad-attribution carry-over across the origin boundary; also platformPageUrl (Hebrew-safe Location)
src/lib/visitor.ts             ← the sn_vid cookie name, TTL and shape — one place, because the handoff re-validates it on the far side of an origin
src/scripts/store-category-picker.ts ← client half of StoreCategoryPicker (near-duplicate suggestions, two-click "add anyway"); convenience only — /api/store.ts re-sanitizes
src/scripts/dashboard/product-multi-picker.ts ← initProductMultiPicker()/readProductOptions() — searchable tick-list of products writing a comma-joined hidden input; rows are built on the client from one JSON blob (a DOM node per product doesn't scale). Used by the boost scope picker
src/scripts/dashboard/product-seo.ts ← initProductSeoHints()/refreshProductSeoPanels() — re-runs product-seo-field.ts's builder as the seller types, delegated at the document so a rebuilt row is covered. Rewrites static text only: no focus move, no scroll, no overlay. The meter is UPDATED (paintMeter) rather than rebuilt, or its fill has no previous width to animate from
src/scripts/dashboard/custom-domain.ts ← settings: connect/verify(+auto-poll)/remove a custom domain (AJAX)
src/scripts/dashboard/store-url.ts ← settings: edit the store URL/slug in place (AJAX, no reload)
src/scripts/dashboard/copy-value.ts ← the one copy-to-clipboard behaviour, delegated on the document (CopyButton.astro)
src/lib/store-products.ts       ← **Postgres since 2026-08-02** (`store_products` + `product_images`/`product_variant_stock`/`product_variant_images`); every reader/writer is async. StoreProduct interface (+`hidden?` seller take-down) + CRUD + isSkuTaken + decrementStock/restockProduct (one conditional UPDATE, returns {ok,before,after} — no mutex) + getEffectiveStock (pure), isProductVisible/getVisibleProductsByStoreId (gates blocked OR hidden), countStockAlerts(storeId,threshold) (Products-tab badge count), getAllProducts() (whole catalog — same open debt as getAllStores), slugify() (keeps letters in ANY script — a Hebrew name used to slug to '' and every product became /store/product-N). Price is ILS here and integer agorot in the column; money.ts#toAgorot/fromAgorot is the boundary. `variantStock` is a PARTIAL map — a combo with no entry sells from the shared pool, which is why `product_variant_stock.stock` is nullable (migration 0003)
src/lib/blank-pixel.ts          ← BLANK_PIXEL 1x1 transparent data URI, shared by the store grid's SSR + JS card templates for deferred (data-src) carousel slides
src/lib/page-view-tap.ts        ← recordPageViewTap({events,visitorId,storeId,productId,productEvent,productIds}) — **everything one page view writes, in ONE statement** (2026-08-03): the funnel event(s) + their session rows + the per-product tally + the store's counter + its unique-visitor row + the product's counter. Never throws. It owns the write SQL for all six tables; the modules named after them own the reads and now call this. Events are DEDUPED (Postgres rejects touching a row twice per command) and a non-uuid productId drops that one write instead of the whole tap. `tests/page-view-tap.test.ts` fails if a second `query(` appears here or if a hot path writes around it
src/lib/product-pageviews.ts    ← **Postgres since 2026-08-02** (`product_page_views`). recordProductView(productId) (a single-intent call into `page-view-tap.ts`, which owns the statement) + getProductViewStats/EMPTY_PRODUCT_VIEW_STATS — per-product TOTAL views (page loads + quick-view opens), keyed by product id. Deliberately NOT a distinct count: a repeat view by the same person counts again, because the drill-down asks how many times a product was looked at
src/lib/analytics.ts            ← **Postgres since 2026-08-02** (`analytics_daily`/`analytics_visitors`/`analytics_products`). recordAnalyticsEvent(type,{vid,productIds}) is the single-event entrance to `page-view-tap.ts`, which owns the statement (one round trip for everything a page view writes, product tally GROUPed before insert). Reads: getEventTotals (a stage is COUNT(DISTINCT visitor_id) over the RANGE — never a sum of days) + getTopProductsByEvent/getTopAbandonedProducts (ranked and LIMITed in the DB, the gap subtracted before the LIMIT) + getAnalyticsOverview + getLifetimeEventSessions. Pure and synchronous over those totals: buildFunnel/buildAnalyticsRates. FUNNEL_EVENTS (page_view→purchase) + AUX_EVENTS (seller_register_view)
src/lib/pricing.ts               ← SELLER_TIERS (monthly fee + commission per tier) + resolveTier/commissionPercentForTier/monthlyFeeForTier/blendedCommissionRate + AD_PLATFORM_MARGIN_PERCENT/boostFeePercent (the ad MARGIN itself is a subtraction, never a percentage — ad-scope-label.ts#campaignFeeOf); pure, the ONLY source of what a seller pays us (store.config deliberately has no commission %)
src/lib/seller-onboarding.ts     ← buildOnboardingSteps/onboardingProgress/missingRequiredSteps — the 5-step dashboard checklist, derived not stored; `required` steps mirror store-readiness.ts
src/lib/store-readiness.ts       ← buildStoreReadiness/isStoreReady — the discovery gate (no visible product = out of sitemap/search + noindex); pure
src/lib/seller-funnel.ts        ← buildSellerFunnel (pure: registered→withStore→withProduct→withSale from records + registerViews) + getSellerFunnel(), which is five COUNTs in one statement since §3 — it used to read every seller, store, product and order on the platform to produce five integers
src/lib/spam-filter.ts          ← findSpamKeyword (word-boundary blocklist) + findKeywordStuffing (repetition/density heuristic), both pure. Guards the shared SEO DOMAIN against keyword stuffing; it is NOT a prohibited-products check — see ad-policy.ts
src/lib/ad-policy.ts            ← adPolicyViolation(): what we may not SUBMIT to Google/Meta, as distinct from what a seller may SELL. Both networks suspend the ACCOUNT for prohibited content and the platform advertises everyone through one, so one vape listing takes every store off both networks. Excludes from the FEED only — the product keeps selling, its page and its SEO. The list is deliberately NARROW because a wrongly-excluded product is a silent harm the seller pays for: knives designed as weapons are prohibited, cutlery is not, and "אקדח" is also a glue gun — so entries are unambiguous phrases, and tests/ad-policy.test.ts pins 13 ordinary products that MUST NOT match. A floor under the owner's own what-may-be-sold decision (GO_LIVE), never a substitute for it
src/lib/store-products-bulk.ts  ← bulkUpsertProducts (CSV batch create/update, preserve-on-blank) + updateChangesProduct (no-op diff for the preview, mirrors the write) + productsToCsv + productsToFeedJson (outbound feed JSON) (needs store-categories.ts → server-only)
src/lib/product-listing.ts      ← filterAndSortProducts (category+search+sort, shared by store SSR + /api/store-products) + rankDefault (the 'default'/"מומלצים" smart order: in-stock → demand-tier → units → newest) + normalizeHe/matchesQueryWords + PRODUCTS_PAGE_SIZE (pure/isomorphic) (tests/product-listing.test.ts)
src/lib/store-products-import.ts ← runProductImport(): the shared CSV→catalog routine (parse→validate→variant-merge→preview/commit + restock-notification cleanup) behind both the bulk API and the feed-sync URL pull; sku-matching runs on every import (id wins, else existing sku, else create); flags no-op updates (updateChangesProduct) and pinpoints which variant combos changed (changedCombos); rejects an option-less row that tries to move a per-combo product's stock
src/lib/store-taxonomy.ts       ← the SHARED store-category vocabulary: SEED_CATEGORIES + buildVocabulary/proposeCategory/findSimilarCategories/sanitizeStoreCategories (pure). Distinct from store-categories.ts below — that one is a single store's PRODUCT tree
src/lib/category-icons.ts       ← categoryIconKey(label) — which icon a PLATFORM category gets (seed map + ALIAS_ICONS for common non-seed labels + findSimilarCategories inheritance + neutral fallback). Pure; artwork lives in CategoryIcon.astro
src/lib/store-categories.ts     ← StoreCategory tree CRUD, resolveCategoryFilterIds, countProductsPerCategory (subtree counts for the store page's chips), categoryUrlParam/findCategoryByParam (the `?category=` value — SLUG, falling back to the id when a name has no sluggable characters at all, e.g. "★★★"; the finder accepts either, so links made before slugs still resolve), resolveOrCreateCategoryPaths (node:fs — never let client-bundled code value-import this)
src/lib/db.ts                   ← the Postgres pool (node-postgres): query/rows/firstRow/withTransaction + isDbConfigured + isUuid/NO_SUCH_UUID (Postgres RAISES on a malformed uuid literal instead of not matching it — shape-check before every by-id query). LAZY — nothing connects until the first query, which is what lets a guard test that never queries stay free. Its header holds the pool-size/timeout reasoning (§7.16: connecting per request is the #1 "worked in dev, fell over in production")
src/lib/record-rev.ts           ← per-FIELD revisions (PRODUCT_REV_FIELDS/STORE_REV_FIELDS → productEditRev/storeSettingsRev) + mergeByFieldRev(): the three-way merge behind the multi-tab guard on /api/product edit-product + /api/store save-settings. Pure/isomorphic, no schema change, survives the DB move (tests/record-rev.test.ts)
src/lib/csv-import-advisory.ts  ← importSeoAdvisory — counts the rows a CSV import would write that end up with no image or no real description, for the preview's advisory note. Pure; counts the RESULT of an update row (a blank cell keeps the stored value), skips error and unchanged rows
src/lib/csv-bulk.ts             ← CSV parse/stringify (3 category cols + group + 3 generic option name/value pairs via OPTION_SLOTS + a trailing weight column — CSV_FIELDS is APPEND-ONLY so an older file still imports, its missing cells reading blank = unchanged), header i18n mapping, row validation incl. orphan-subcategory check, templateCsv, resolveSkuMatches (sku→id pre-pass) (pure, isomorphic)
src/lib/variant-csv.ts          ← mergeVariantGroups(): collapses rows sharing a group value OR a repeated existing id into one variant product (generic option name/value dims → variants/variantStock/variantSku per comboKey; carries per-combo line+label so the preview can point at the changed row) — pure/isomorphic, runs after validateRows
src/lib/feed-mapping.ts         ← external-inventory feed translator: guessMapping (he/en header synonyms → canonical keys, saved-mapping override) for the SELLER, confirmedMapping (saved mapping only, no synonym fallback) for the TIMER — an unmapped column must not gain a say when nobody is previewing; buildCanonicalCsv, mappingStatus — pure/isomorphic
src/lib/feed-fetch.ts           ← fetchFeedCsv(url): SSRF-guarded server pull for the external inventory feed — protocol + resolved-IP allowlist, and the vetted address is PINNED onto the socket (assertSafeUrl → fetchPinned via a `lookup` hook), which closes the DNS-rebinding window and is why it uses node:http/https rather than fetch; refuses redirects, caps size mid-stream, sends no Accept-Encoding. Server-only
src/lib/store-feed-sync.ts      ← syncStoreFeed(store, commit, trigger): one store's external-feed pull end to end (fetch behind the SSRF guard → apply the column mapping → runProductImport as the store's OWN seller). Shared by the seller's "sync now" route and the scheduler's feed-sync job, which has no session to present. trigger='scheduled' infers NOTHING: confirmed mapping only, and it refuses a feed with no sku/id column rather than re-creating the whole catalogue every hour. The write is absolute (sets stock, never adds), which is what makes a double-run harmless. syncedRowCount() = what a run actually wrote
src/lib/jobs/scheduler.ts       ← the periodic trigger: an unref'd in-process timer that calls runDueJobs() (the seam — safe to call from anywhere, so swapping to an external cron changes only the trigger). Started from the first request via ensureSchedulerStarted() (Astro has no server-start hook). **The default is the BUILD**: `npm start` runs jobs, `npm run dev` does not — .env holds real Neon credentials, so a DATABASE_URL-based default had the dev server claiming them (the one sanctioned import.meta.env.PROD read; "am I the dev server" is build-time by definition). SCHEDULER_ENABLED overrides both ways (1=on in dev, 0=off in a built instance); off without DATABASE_URL either way. Per-job in-flight guard, failures go to the admin Alerts tab. **Every run is bounded by its own `leaseSec` and ABANDONED past it** — a job that HANGS is not a job that throws: it used to hold its name in the in-flight set for the life of the process and never call finishJob, so it stopped forever while its row kept the previous run's `last_status` ('ok'). The lease is the right deadline because past it another instance may already overlap; the zombie keeps running, which is safe only because every job in registry.ts is required to be idempotent
src/lib/jobs/job-runs.ts        ← the durable claim: claimJob (one INSERT…ON CONFLICT — due AND unleased, affected-row count is the verdict) / finishJob / getJobRun. next_run_at survives restarts so a deploy does not re-run everything; lease_until stops a run outliving its own interval from being started twice
src/lib/jobs/registry.ts        ← JOBS: the periodic jobs and their intervals — purge-checkouts (6h), purge-auth-attempts (6h, size only — an expired row is already invisible to checkAuthRate), campaign-sweep (15m, calls getCampaignsForStore so the pause/resume rules exist once), feed-sync (1h, capped at 200 longest-unsynced stores and announces the cap, skips a store that may not sell via canStoreSell), custom-domain-check (1h, 50 at a time), merchant-status (4h, inert without ad-network credentials), feed-artifact + sitemap-artifact (30m each — they pre-build the two public catalogue documents that used to be assembled inside a request; the interval IS the routes' max-age, catalog-artifacts.ts), order-sla (24h — the only job that CANCELS anything and the only one that gives money back to a person: warns on day 7, cancels + restocks + records refund_due on day 14, through order-status-change.ts and never a second copy of the cancellation), purge-visitor-detail (24h — the only job that deletes rows the app still displays inside its window; window and cutoff in visitor-retention.ts, and it SKIPS AUX_EVENTS because getLifetimeEventSessions is unbounded by design). Every job must be idempotent and must not throw. tests/jobs-scheduler.test.ts asserts the list WHOLE, so a job added without a written idempotency argument fails there rather than shipping quietly
src/lib/product-weight.ts       ← MAX_WEIGHT_GRAMS + parseWeightGrams/feedShippingWeight. Whole grams, and the rule everything else rests on: "not stated" is NOT zero — a zero-gram parcel is a ₪0 carrier quote, so NULL column + CHECK > 0, the feed omits the attribute rather than publishing "0 g", and an unusable CSV weight FAILS its row. Collected before a carrier consumes it, deliberately (migrations/0011_product_weight.sql says why)
src/lib/product-form.ts         ← parseImages/Category/Sku/Weight/Tags/Specs/VariantsPayload — shared by /api/product.ts; parseImages runs every URL through image-url.ts (was verbatim)
src/lib/arrow-step.ts           ← arrowStep()/wrapIndex() — THE horizontal-arrow-key rule. An arrow names a direction ON SCREEN, so in RTL (this site's default) ArrowRight must walk BACKWARD through source order; returns +1/-1/0 off the element's computed `direction`, not the language. Shipped inverted in the homepage tab strip + 2 lightboxes while the product page solved it inline — tests/arrow-step.test.ts greps src/ and fails any new 'ArrowRight'/'ArrowLeft' comparison that skips it
src/lib/variant-combo.ts        ← comboKey()/generateCombos()/realDimensions()/resolveSelection()/canonicalDimName()/isProductInStock()/comboStockRows()/isFullyPerCombo()/sumComboOverrides()/LOW_STOCK_THRESHOLD + comboCount()/exceedsComboLimit()/MAX_VARIANT_COMBOS (2026-08-06 — the cartesian product is exponential while the payload describing it is linear, so a few KB of JSON asked for 125k combos and parseVariantsPayload BUILT them before validating; both input gates now count instead of expanding) — pure/isomorphic, shared by variant editor + storefront + checkout threshold + inline per-combo stock edit. `variantStock` is PARTIAL: a combo with no entry sells from the shared pool, so comboStockRows() reports override/effective/shared rather than completing the map. `evenSplit`/`resolveVariantStockMap` were REMOVED 2026-08-02 — they invented a per-combo breakdown nobody entered
src/lib/color-variants.ts       ← resolveVariantColor()/isColorVariant(); exports COLOR_VARIANT_NAME_GROUP (reused by variant-combo.ts's canonicalDimName)
src/lib/variant-landing.ts      ← variantLandingUrl()/parseVariantLanding()/VARIANT_PARAM — the `?v=<comboKey>` a variant feed row lands on, and the selection the product page reads back. A variant product emits NO parent row, so every row used to publish the SAME link onto a page that pre-selects nothing: a paid click for red/42 arrived with nothing chosen. The value is the comboKey VERBATIM (same string the row id and variantStock key on — a second spelling is a second thing to sync). Every unknown pair is dropped and dropping is never an error: the failure mode is the page it would have rendered anyway
src/lib/discounts.ts            ← THE price resolver: resolvePrice/effectivePrice over the two levers (per-product discount + store sale), lowest wins; pure/isomorphic (runs in the browser too). Also activeDiscountWindow() — "which lever won, and over what dates", asked ONCE so the page's expiry and the feed's date range cannot name different sales — and priceValidUntil() on top of it, the day AFTER endsAt because endsAt is inclusive
src/lib/discount-input.ts       ← untrusted-input side: form/CSV/JSON → typed ProductDiscount/StoreSale (percent clamped 1–95, ₪-off bounded by price, text capped); kept out of discounts.ts so that stays pure math
src/lib/discount-field.ts       ← the seller's per-product discount block as one HTML string — the product edit form exists twice (SSR + client rebuild), so both render from here
src/components/dashboard/HeaderStyleCard.astro ← Settings "ראש החנות": both header lockups as live previews, built from the header's OWN classes; the logo radio is disabled until an upload exists
src/scripts/dashboard/btn-busy.ts ← busyButton() — THE in-flight state for a dashboard button: `.dot-pulse` + `.btn--busy` (cursor:progress, which is not "disabled") + a live percentage, and it restores innerHTML so a button's inline icon survives the action. Extracted from store-image.ts the day header-logo.ts needed the same eight lines
src/components/dashboard/ImageActionIcon.astro ← the crop-frame and wand icons for "התאם תמונה"/"הסר רקע", shared by the banner, avatar and header-logo buttons
src/scripts/dashboard/header-logo.ts ← initHeaderLogoCard() — upload / background-removal / remove for the header logo, and the one `sync()` that keeps the radio, preview, placeholder and buttons saying the same thing. No crop flow: a logo's aspect ratio IS the logo
src/lib/coupons.ts              ← coupon codes, pure/isomorphic: normalizeCouponCode (upper-case A–Z0–9-, a KEEP list so it is not read as the slug rule), isCouponLive (offerable at all — what decides whether a buyer sees a field), checkCoupon/couponDiscountAgorot (clamped to [0, subtotal]), publicCoupon (what a buyer may learn). NOT a third price lever — a coupon never touches a published price
src/lib/coupon-input.ts         ← untrusted seller form → CouponWrite or a named refusal; percent to the shared 1–95 band, ₪-off/threshold/uses bounded, a window that closes before it opens refused
src/lib/store-coupons.ts        ← the DB side: CRUD scoped by store_id, storesWithLiveCoupons (ONE query for a whole cart, uuid-filtered), and claimCoupon/releaseCoupon — a conditional UPDATE whose affected-row count IS the answer, the decrementStock shape, which is what makes "first 50" mean 50
src/lib/product-seo-hints.ts    ← productSeoHints/openProductSeoHints/needsSeoAttention/productSeoScore — per-product discoverability advice, derived + state-free like seller-onboarding.ts. Only the missing image is `required` (the ad feed drops such a product); needsSeoAttention is deliberately NOT "any hint open" — it is what the products table's `weak` band and its filter both mean
src/lib/product-seo-field.ts    ← that advice + a fill-bar meter (productSeoMeterView, one token per band) + a search-result preview as one HTML string — same SSR/client-rebuild reason as discount-field.ts. Also productSeoRowGaugeHtml, the products-table row dial (same score, same three colours, same band words as the meter). Advice only: no overlay, no link, nothing disabled, so it can never strand a seller mid-form
src/lib/price-html.ts           ← sale price + badge markup as a string; ~8 surfaces, half built client-side, so a component alone would cover only half and the rest would drift
src/lib/store-sale-scope.ts     ← flattens a sale's category scope (each chosen category + its descendants, union) and STORES it on the sale, because resolvePrice runs where the tree isn't available; also owns the banner's scope wording
src/lib/sale-scope-label.ts     ← pure wording rule for a sale's category scope (names, tail past 2 → "ועוד N") — shared by the banner, the dashboard preview and the picker label so one sale can't be worded three ways
src/lib/own-store-guard.ts      ← isOwnStore/blockOwnStorePurchase — refuses add-to-cart on the logged-in seller's OWN store (client half). The server half is /api/checkout's 403 `own-store`, and it identifies the buyer by SESSION **and** by the email typed at checkout (2026-08-05) — session alone missed the likeliest case, a seller testing his own checkout signed out from a phone, for whom an accidental first sale starts his monthly fee
src/lib/safe-redirect.ts        ← safeRedirectPath/safeRefererPath — the ONLY place deciding whether a request-supplied destination may be redirected to; used by login/register/logout/api-auth-google(+callback)/api-lang. Never hand-roll the `startsWith('//')` check again — tests/safe-redirect.test.ts fails the build if a route does. It also fails a `form.get('_next')` that skips the helper: `?next=` was sanitised everywhere while the HIDDEN field the same forms carry was not, and a hidden input is exactly as attacker-controlled (login + register both put it straight into a Location until 2026-08-06)
src/lib/store-ownership.ts      ← ownedStore/ownedProduct — the ONLY place a seller's claim on a store id or a product id is settled. Every catalogue-mutating page goes through it; tests/store-ownership.test.ts fails any file under src/pages that reaches createProduct/updateProduct/deleteProduct without it, or that re-scans getStoresBySellerId inline (the copy-paste shape that left seller/dashboard.astro's no-JS handlers with no check at all)
src/lib/runtime-env.ts          ← serverEnv/requiredSecret — the ONLY way to read a server-side env var, from the live process.env. `import.meta.env.NAME` is a build-time TEXT SUBSTITUTION: built without the var, the constant folder collapsed seller-auth's whole guard to an unconditional throw, so every session check on the deployed server failed whatever its environment said. Also hydrateProcessEnv (astro.config.mjs loads .env for the dev server, which Astro doesn't). PUBLIC_* stays on import.meta.env — it is inlined into browser bundles on purpose. tests/runtime-env.test.ts scans src/
src/lib/google-oauth.ts         ← googleClientId/ClientSecret/RedirectUri + isGoogleAuthEnabled — one owner for "is Google sign-in configured", because the two routes read the same 3 vars inline and the login/register pages had no way to ask: they rendered "המשך עם Google" while the route answered a bare 503
src/lib/secret-compare.ts       ← secretsEqual — the ONLY way to compare a signature/password/token. `===` stops at the first differing character, so it leaks how much of a guess was right; compares sha256 digests because timingSafeEqual throws on unequal lengths and the throw leaks the length. tests/secret-compare.test.ts greps src/ and fails if a route hand-rolls it
src/lib/csrf-names.ts           ← the header/field/meta names, and nothing else. Its own module because the two ends run in different runtimes and cannot import each other (csrf.ts pulls node:crypto + the pool), so the strings were written out twice — and renaming the header on the server would have made every mutating request a silent 403
src/lib/csrf.ts                 ← the signed-token CSRF layer: issueCsrfToken/verifyCsrfToken (HMAC over `<binding>|<expiry>`, no storage) + csrfRequired/csrfTokenFromRequest/csrfRejection. Bound to the SESSION — an unbound token only proves the sender once visited the site, which an attacker does for themselves. Enforced ONLY in src/middleware.ts; tests/csrf.test.ts greps src/ so it cannot spread to a route. Its header names the two layers underneath it (sameSite:'lax' cookies, Astro's checkOrigin — pinned explicitly in astro.config.mjs because the default is conditional on buildOutput)
src/lib/rate-limit.ts           ← the shared fixed-window limiter (auth_attempts). reportRules is the one rule that counts SUCCESSES (a flood of valid reports is the abuse; there is no secret to guess). Failed-sign-in throttling: sellerLoginRules/registerRules/adminLoginRules build the buckets, checkAuthRate (read-only) → countAuthAttempt / clearAuthRate. Fixed window, failures only, one atomic INSERT…ON CONFLICT so parallel guesses cannot both read the same count. Identity key is lower-cased AND length-capped — the cap is load-bearing, see its comment. MAX_RATE_WINDOW_SEC is the purge job's cut-off and is NOT this file's own window: it purged by the 15-minute auth window, which would have deleted message-flood.ts's hour-long buckets at minute 16.
src/lib/client-ip.ts            ← clientIp(request, socketAddress) for rate limiting only. Trusts cf-connecting-ip/x-forwarded-for ONLY when TRUST_PROXY_IP is set (a header is a claim); normalises ::ffff: and caps length. Both directions of misconfiguration are documented in the file.
src/lib/shutdown.ts             ← trackRequest()/ensureShutdownHookInstalled(), both called from middleware.ts. Drains in-flight requests on SIGTERM before exiting. What it does and does not cover is on the file.
src/lib/email-address.ts        ← one definition of "is this an email address", length cap BEFORE pattern (the cap is what makes it ReDoS-safe, not the regex)
src/lib/request-body.ts         ← readJsonBody(request, BODY_LIMIT.*) — the ONLY way an API route reads a JSON body; readFormBody() is the same bounded read for a `request.clone()` whose body somebody else still has to read (the CSRF gate), and it must NOT cancel on overflow — cancel() on a tee'd branch never settles while the other branch lives, which would hang the middleware. Counts the bytes as they arrive instead of trusting `Content-Length`, which a chunked request simply omits (that check read 0 and waved everything through). BODY_LIMIT is a four-word vocabulary — control/form/collection/upload — so 28 call sites don't each invent a number. Never call `request.json()` in a route; `tests/request-body.test.ts` greps for both mistakes
src/lib/url-base.ts             ← THE slug rule + linear-time string edges: toSlug (NFKC → letters of any script/digits/`-`, 120-char cap; stores.ts#normalizeSlug and store-products.ts#slugify are both this) · slugChars (same class, keeps spaces — the live-typing fields) · stripTrailingSlashes + trimDashes (`/\/+$/` and `^-+|-+$` are quadratic on request input) · urlSegment (percent-encode a Hebrew slug for sitemap/feed/canonical) · machineUrl (idempotent whole-URL/path encode for a HEADER — `Astro.params` is decoded and a raw Hebrew Location THROWS a 500; every interpolated redirect uses it). tests/url-base.test.ts MEASURES elapsed time + greps for re-hand-rolling
src/lib/slug-confusable.ts      ← confusableSkeleton(): folds Cyrillic/Greek letters that LOOK Latin onto their twin, so isReservedSlug catches `аdmin` with a Cyrillic а. Owner decision 2026-08-02 — it guards reserved ROUTES only; seller-vs-seller lookalikes are a report/rule-violation matter, and mixing scripts stays legal (`shop-ישראל` is a normal slug)
src/lib/lightbox-image.ts       ← full-screen image loading shared by all three lightboxes (product page, StoreProductModal, ProductQuickView) — blur-up preview, delayed dot-pulse pill, lightboxWidth (the 800/1200/1600 rung the screen needs), warmLightboxImage on hover, neighbourIndexes (±1 preload)
src/lib/image-derive.ts         ← warmImageDerivations / warmBannerDerivations: HEADs a saved product's images at LIGHTBOX_WIDTHS, and a saved store's banner at BANNER_WIDTHS, so the CDN renders them at SAVE time rather than under the first visitor. Cloudinary renders a transform lazily: ~0.80s cold, ~0.19s warm (2026-08-03) — and the banner is the store page's LCP element, so that was the worst place on the site to pay it. Fire-and-forget like indexnow.ts. Single-product save paths only — bulk is excluded on purpose (see the file header)
src/lib/gallery-widget.ts       ← gallery HTML helper
src/lib/ad-item-id.ts           ← adItemId(productId, selectedVariants?) / adComboItemId(productId, comboKey) — THE id Google and Meta know an item by, built once for both sides of a join that only works when they agree. Always the product uuid (never the slug: unique per store only, and it follows the name); narrowed to the chosen combo because a variant product emits one feed row PER COMBO and no parent row. Over Google's 50-char `id` cap (uuid 36 + a Hebrew combo) the combo is HASHED, never truncated — that would collapse two combos onto one id. tests/ad-item-id.test.ts cross-checks it against the real feed builder; tests/ad-item-id-call-sites.test.ts greps src/ so no call site invents its own
src/lib/tracking.ts             ← trackViewContent, trackAddToCart, trackInitiateCheckout (checkout.astro, once per page load, AFTER the re-price — `value` is what Meta optimises against, so it must be the price that will be charged). `value` goes through sumMoney, never an inline round. **`TrackItem` carries TWO ids and they are not interchangeable:** `id` = the catalog id from ad-item-id.ts (what GTM/fbq get, may carry a combo suffix), `productId` = the raw uuid (what our own /api/analytics/event gets, and it accepts ONLY a uuid — sending the other silently recorded every add_to_cart with no product for as long as this file existed). The two halves are reported independently, so a gap in one never costs the other its event. Otherwise these push to GTM/fbq ONLY; our own funnel counts the same stages server-side in middleware.ts, so a first-party fetch beside InitiateCheckout would double-count it
src/lib/attribution.ts          ← first-party ad attribution (GO_LIVE §2.5 layer 5): captureAttribution(url, cookies) in middleware.ts writes gclid/gbraid/wbraid/fbclid/utm_* off the landing URL into the httpOnly `sn_attr` cookie; readAttribution(cookies) in /api/checkout stamps it on every order of the checkout (orders.attribution, migration 0010). Last click REPLACES whole (a merge credits two networks with one sale); a landing with no params leaves the cookie alone (that visitor is what the window measures); the 30-day window is enforced entering the cookie and NOT leaving storage (sanitizeAttribution) — an old order keeps its click. Nothing reads the column yet, on purpose: until real ad accounts exist no link carries the params
src/lib/user-carts.ts           ← ALL buyer state, one module over four tables: cart lines, wishlist, saved stores, recently-visited. getUserCart/replaceUserCart (the sync endpoint's snapshot, written as a diff so a qty change is one UPDATE of one row) + narrow writers removeCartLines/toggleFavoriteStore + the two counts that used to be stored files (countFavoriteStores/getWishlistCountsForStore = COUNT(*), §5). cart_items keys by TEXT store_slug+cart_key with no FK ON PURPOSE (a line whose product was deleted must stay visible and marked); wishlist/favourites key by uuid, because a bare product slug is not an identity (§7.1)
src/lib/cart-sync.ts            ← merge guest→user, debounced save
src/lib/orders.ts               ← **A ROUTE MAY NOT READ A STORE'S WHOLE HISTORY** (`tests/public-route-unbounded-build.test.ts` scans `src/pages/`): five did, to draw fifteen rows, one month's chart or a 15s poll. Three bounded readers are the vocabulary — `getOrdersByStoreSlugInRange` (a WINDOW; half-open on the raw column so the index survives), `getSellerOrdersPage` (a PAGE: filter/search/sort/count/slice in one query, twinned with seller-orders-query.ts by a parity test) and `getSellerOrdersSince` (a WATERMARK: `>=` and the caller de-dupes, or two orders written in one transaction lose one). `getOrdersByStoreSlug` stays for tests/scripts. Order interfaces + CRUD + orderBelongsToStore (EVERY by-id order mutation binds the id to the caller's store through it — a session proves which stores, never which orders) + buyerId + getPurchasedCountsByStoreSlug (productId→units, Products tab's "נרכשו" column); StoreSubtotal.deliveryMethod (chosen delivery method per store); createOrder takes paymentStatus from caller (set from PaymentProvider result); **countsAsRevenue(order) is THE definition of "money actually earned"** — paid AND not cancelled — because a cancellation deliberately leaves paymentStatus:'paid' and only moves shippingStatus, so a bare paid-check kept cancelled+restocked orders inside every revenue sum (fixed סשן ה׳). **`getPurchasedCountsByStoreSlug` rides the same predicate** — it summed EVERY order (pending/failed/cancelled included), and it is not just the Products tab's "נרכשו" column: it also orders the public storefront by popularity and becomes `custom_label_1` (performanceTier) in the Merchant/Meta feed, so phantom units pulled real campaign budget
src/lib/order-reporting.ts      ← every WHOLE-PLATFORM question about orders, as a query (§3, 2026-08-03): getPlatformOrderTotals (the Overview headline), getStoreRevenueBySlug, getOpenOrderCountsByStore, countOrdersSince, getAllOrderStoreNames, getPlatformSales (per-store per-bucket sales + a true platform top-N). The revenue rule is never spelled out in SQL — order-status-rules.ts's two columns are passed in as list parameters; NET is `GREATEST(subtotal − discount, 0)`, the SQL half of admin-stats#orderNetForStore; the day/month bucket is `AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Jerusalem'`, never the server's calendar
src/lib/shipping.ts             ← pure/isomorphic delivery model (client+server): DeliveryMethod, SHIPPING_RATES (platform-set courier/pickup_point), availableDeliveryMethods/shippingPrice/normalizeDeliveryMethod (server re-validation guard) + offerShippingDetails() (schema.org OfferShippingDetails from the same rates; deliveryTime deliberately absent until a carrier gives real numbers — GO_LIVE §5)
src/lib/payment.ts              ← PaymentProvider (authorize/capture/voidCharge) + MockPaymentProvider; paymentProvider singleton — swap for the real Israeli split-provider at go-live. HEADER IS THE ARGUMENT: hold first, write orders, capture last, so an order exists iff money moved. Approves everything EXCEPT a buyer email containing MOCK_DECLINE_MARKER (`+decline@`, DEV-gated). PaymentRequest carries idempotencyKey through to the gateway
src/lib/money.ts                ← roundMoney/sumMoney/percentOf — THE place an amount is rounded; every summed/stored/returned/charged figure goes through it. Interim: real fix is integer agorot at the DB migration
src/lib/business-day.ts         ← THE answer to "which calendar day/month did this happen on". businessDayISO/businessMonthKey for a real INSTANT (Asia/Jerusalem, DST-safe); calendarDayISO/calendarMonthKey for a synthetic axis cursor — **confusing the two families IS the bug it exists to prevent**. dayInRange compares business-day strings (no instant arithmetic); businessOffsetForDay() gives a day's ±hhmm for the one case that must hand a business day OUTSIDE as a real instant (the feed's sale window)
src/lib/order-status-rules.ts   ← the status matrix: one row per shipping/payment status, one column per consequence (countsAsRevenue/holdsStock/cancellableFrom/terminal/notifiesBuyer/buyerAwaiting/blocksStoreClosure/payoutClockRuns/sellerOwesAction — the last one is "whose turn is it", deliberately NOT `!payoutClockRuns`, which selects the same three rows today for a different reason) + canTransition/orderHoldsStock/CANCELLABLE_FROM. A new status fills a row, never a new `if` at a call site; a test parses the Order type and fails if a status has no row
src/lib/money-events.ts         ← append-only journal of everything that moves money (charge attempts incl. declines, order created, duplicate blocked, status/amount changes, refund_due/refund_settled). Never throws — a failed journal write must not fail the operation it records. Admin "יומן כספי" tab. `getMoneyEventsPage`/`moneyEventPage` are what the panel uses: narrowing, search, count and page slice ALL in the query. The date window is a HALF-OPEN range on the raw column — a function on `at` took `money_events_at_idx` out of the plan and left a seq scan of the one table nothing is ever deleted from (300k rows: 149ms + 12,747 rows over the wire → 17ms + 15). `getMoneyEvents` stays for the tests and the parity guard
src/lib/money-event-types.ts    ← the journal's VOCABULARY only (types, Hebrew labels, isMoneyEventType), split out so the SQL search builder and money-events.ts can both have it without importing each other. money-events.ts re-exports it all
src/lib/moneylog-search.ts      ← the admin journal's free-text search, as SQL. Two things it matches do not exist in the DB: the Hebrew LABEL of a type (resolved to `type` values in JS, before the query) and the amount as RENDERED (reproduced in SQL — `349.9` is a substring of `2,349.90`, so no candidate set can be enumerated). `amountGuards` are cheap arithmetic conditions that prune, or prove, the expensive rendering arm away. Held against the in-memory reference by tests/moneylog-search-parity.test.ts
src/lib/admin-moneylog-panel.ts ← everything the money-journal tab renders, assembled from the URL. States its own query chain once: no permalink = ONE round trip, `?mev=` = three (the row's day → its rank → the page)
src/lib/admin-tab-badges.ts     ← the "(N)" on every admin tab, as ONE statement of five COUNTs. Fetched on every request whatever panel is open — the badge exists to report a tab you are NOT looking at, so it must not be derived from a list that panel happens to load
src/lib/refund-owed.ts          ← when a status move leaves the BUYER owed money (a captured payment that stops counting as revenue), and the `refund_due` entry that says so. Asks order-status-rules#moneyWasTaken, never the word 'cancelled'
src/lib/order-status-change.ts  ← every consequence of a shipping-status move, in ONE place: journal → refund obligation → restock → buyer notification → pending store closure. `settleStatusChange` for a caller that did its own updateOrder (the seller route), `moveOrderStatus` for one with no route around it (the SLA job; the courier webhook next). Never throws — the write it reports on is already persisted
src/lib/order-sla-run.ts        ← the job behind order-sla.ts: warns the seller on day 7, cancels + refunds on day 14, through moveOrderStatus and never a second copy of it. SQL narrows to a necessary condition (a day wide, business calendar vs raw timestamp); fulfilmentStatus decides. Warning deduplicated against the seller's own notification feed under an `sla-late:` key — the bare order id already carries the new_order notice
src/lib/payout-run.ts           ← planPayouts() decides who gets paid on the next payout day, how much and why not (payable/below_minimum/no_bank/already_paid/settled); runPayouts() is that plan plus the rows. Split so the admin can SEE the coming transfer without a second definition of who gets paid — four queries whatever the platform's size, because a monthly job can afford 2N reads and a screen cannot. nextPayoutDayISO does string arithmetic on a day key, never Date maths
src/components/admin/AdminPayoutPlanCard.astro ← the admin `תשלומים למוכרים` tab: what leaves the company's account on the payout day, what is stuck with no bank details, what is below the minimum — and beside them `כסף בדרך למוכרים`, the held money split by reason (payouts.ts#getHeldBreakdown), each tile linking into the orders behind it. No per-seller table: those facts live on the seller cards (סשן א׳ §3)
src/scripts/admin/payouts.ts    ← one call to wirePanelLinks, so that tab's pager swaps the panel rather than navigating. A panel with no module has NO interception — wirePanelLinks is called by each panel's own module, never by anything shared
src/lib/payout-details.ts       ← PURE validation + normalisation of the seller's bank and business fields, plus hasPayableBank/maskedBankLine/businessSummaryLine (the closed summary's business line, assembled HERE because the panel and the post-save patch both draw it). Bank is all four or none (payout-run.ts#bankOf cannot transfer on three); business id is 9 digits and its check digit is deliberately not enforced — the header says why a false rejection is unrecoverable from the seller's side
src/lib/order-payout-line.ts    ← ONE order's payout status as a screen must say it: state, release day, WHY, and whether the seller is blocking their own money. Returns translation KEYS, never Hebrew — two of its three renderers run in the browser. Also splitHeldByBasis (the payments tab's reason table, scoped to one store), releasedForStore, payableHeadlineAgorot (the "תשלום קרוב" headline BOTH the payments tab and the overview print — this shop's released money for a multi-store seller, the account figure for a single-store one; two different quantities behind one label, which is why it is a function and not two lines in a component) and payoutFilterValue (the Orders tab's `סטטוס תשלום` filter column). A presentation projection of payout-hold.ts, never a second copy of it
src/lib/reconcile.ts            ← recomputes each money headline by an INDEPENDENT second route and reports the drift. TWO routes: reconcileOrders (pure, over an order array — what reporting-fuzz drives) and reconcilePlatform (the same checks as queries returning ONLY the rows that disagree, capped at 50 with a `truncated` flag).
```
