Both build their findings from the same builder functions, and reporting-invariants requires the same verdict — over the SHARED checks: `JOURNAL_ONLY_CHECKS` (an unsettled refund, an order stuck before its capture, a charge with no order) read money_events, which a pure function over orders cannot know
src/lib/checkout-idempotency.ts ← double-charge guard: claimCheckout→completeCheckout/releaseCheckout over a TTL'd ledger, key bound to the buyer (checkoutOwner). The claim is ONE conditional INSERT…ON CONFLICT whose affected-row count is the verdict, so it is correct on any number of instances — the single-process Mutex is deleted. purgeExpiredCheckouts() is a scheduled job (src/lib/jobs/)
src/lib/checkout-attempt-key.ts ← client half of the same guard: one key per checkout ATTEMPT in sessionStorage, deliberately SURVIVING a failed submit (the lost-response retry is the case most likely to double-charge), cleared only on confirmed success
src/lib/messages.ts             ← Message interface + CRUD + markRead
src/lib/message-flood.ts        ← anti-flood for buyer↔seller messaging, SYMMETRIC: a per-thread run cap (unansweredRun ≥ MAX_UNANSWERED_IN_THREAD, needs no storage) + rate buckets via rate-limit.ts (msg-open per store, msg-send overall). Counts SUCCESSES, unlike every other caller of that limiter — its header says why, and why the windows must stay ≤ MAX_RATE_WINDOW_SEC
src/lib/seller-alerts.ts        ← byStore → 'danger'|'warning' + needsBank. Per-store danger (pending orders, unread msgs incl. replies), per-store warning (low stock, a boost stalled on a human), ACCOUNT-wide (released money, no bank account). `getSellerAlerts`/`sellerHasAnyAlert` ask the DANGER half only — that pair runs in the site header on every page, and amber promoted there would be lit permanently; `getSellerStoreAlerts` is the full map, for the switcher. The chain has to reach the field that needs doing, or the top of it is just an alarm
src/lib/notifications.ts        ← Notification interface + CRUD; NotificationType incl. 'admin_message'
src/lib/notification-copy.ts    ← the two stored message-notification titles, Hebrew-only BY CONSTRUCTION (written for the recipient, whose language is unknown at write time). Must never ask the dictionary for anything — that is what keeps /api/messages.ts's flood copy bilingual and these two out of scope
src/lib/admin-auth.ts           ← isAdminRequest/requireAdmin/checkAdminPassword/setAdminCookie/clearAdminCookie (admin_token cookie, path:'/'). Hardened 2026-07-30: ADMIN_SECRET missing in PROD throws instead of defaulting to 'admin', the cookie carries a SIGNED EXPIRY not the secret itself, and the signing key is derived (`secret::admin-session`) so the login password isn't also the HMAC key
src/lib/admin-stats.ts          ← the admin dashboard's PURE arithmetic and nothing else: getStoreOverview/getOrderTotals/getSellerCards/attachProducts/getStoresNeedingAttention/getStoreRows/filterSellerCards/filterStoreRows/getStoreRevenueMap/orderNetForStore/orderNetTotal (reused by seller-performance.ts). Everything that was a GROUP BY written as a loop moved to order-reporting.ts (§3, 2026-08-03); what stayed takes pre-fetched data, is synchronous, and is the twin reporting-invariants checks each query against. **`order.storeSubtotals` is optional on legacy rows: guard it where you ITERATE it too, not only where you read a key** — `Object.keys(o.storeSubtotals)` threw on such a row and took down the whole admin Overview / store table / seller Performance render instead of contributing 0 (fixed 2026-07-30)
src/lib/admin-messages.ts       ← AdminMessage (flat per-seller) + summarizeAdminThreads (pure) + getAdminThreadSummaries
src/lib/admin-orders-filter.ts  ← parseOrderQuery + SHIPPING_SORT_ORDER (derived from order-status-rules, never re-listed) + sortOrderStoreNames, and filterAndSortOrders, which is no longer on the render path but IS the unit-testable twin of orders.ts#getAdminOrdersPage — tests/admin-orders-page.test.ts runs both over the same rows
src/lib/admin-nav.ts            ← buildAdminUrl()/encodeList()/decodeList()/debounce()/swapPanel()/wirePanelLinks()/wirePopstateReload() — shared by admin/index.astro (server) + admin client scripts so pager/nav URLs never drift. swap/wire* are the in-panel-AJAX mechanics. `wirePanelLinks` only intercepts *same-tab* `/admin?` links — a cross-tab link falls through to real navigation so the active tab actually switches; `swapPanel` skips its `pushState` when the panel went hidden mid-fetch. Also `ADMIN_TAB_PARAMS`/`stripForeignTabParams()` — the one registry of which query param belongs to which tab (see the Admin dashboard feature bullet), guarded by tests/admin-tab-params.test.ts. encodeList/decodeList/debounce also reused by the seller dashboard's pagination.
src/lib/admin-tab-views.ts      ← getAllLastViewedAt()/recordTabView()/countSince() — per-tab "last left" timestamps backing the "(N) new" badges + the per-row "חדש" marks (the keyed `app_settings` jsonb row, not a table of its own — the file's header says why); /api/admin/tab-view is the only caller of recordTabView
src/scripts/admin/tab-nav.ts    ← initAdminTabNav() — overview stat-card→tab jump (data-goto-panel) + POST /api/admin/tab-view for the tab being LEFT (never entered), incl. a pagehide `fetch(…, {keepalive:true})` so closing the page still counts as leaving (was sendBeacon; a beacon cannot carry the CSRF header and would have needed an exemption) + the URL cleanup that drops the other tabs' query params on every switch
src/lib/admin-moneylog-filter.ts ← parseMoneyLogQuery/filterMoneyEvents/eventPage/hasActiveMoneyLogFilters — money-journal tab's server-side search (mq, ANDed terms, capped at 200 chars) + business-day window (mfrom/mto, inverted range is swapped) + which page holds a `?mev=` permalink. Type narrowing stays in getMoneyEvents; everything per-order/day is a filter over its result
src/scripts/admin/moneylog.ts   ← initAdminMoneyLogPanel() — debounced search + date-range portal + per-row permalink copy + scroll-to the `?mev=` row; every control rewrites the query params and swapPanels (nothing DOM-filters — only one page of rows exists)
src/pages/api/admin/tab-view.ts ← admin-guarded POST, records a tab as just-viewed (recordTabView)
src/pages/api/admin/tab-badges.ts ← admin-guarded GET, the five tab counts as JSON, `no-store`. Reads the boundary and never advances it; polled so a badge is current for the admin who sits on one tab for an hour
src/scripts/admin/lazy-panels.ts ← initLazyAdminPanels() — fills a tab the first time it is opened, via the same swapPanel every filter and pager here uses. Claims `data-lazy` BEFORE the fetch so a double click cannot start two loads; asks for `/admin?panel=X` with no params, since anything in the address bar belongs to the tab being left
src/scripts/admin/tab-badges.ts ← initAdminTabBadges()/acknowledgeTabLeft() — polls the badges every 45s (paused while the document is hidden) and sequences them against the tab-view write with a generation counter: a response that left before that write landed carries the old count and would put back the badge just cleared
src/lib/order-totals.ts         ← storeSliceTotalAgorot() — what one store's slice of an order came to: goods + shipping − the seller's discount. THE definition; five surfaces computed it inline and three dropped the discount (seller card SSR + client, amount sort, buyer order history). NOT admin-stats#orderNetForStore, which is revenue and so excludes shipping
src/lib/buyer-purchases.ts      ← groupBuyerPurchases() — checkout writes one order ROW per store (seller isolation); the buyer's list AND the admin Orders tab show one CARD per purchase. Keys via lib/checkout-group.ts, sums the slice totals, headlines the least-advanced live slice. Display-only: the rows stay split
src/lib/buyer-orders-query.ts   ← filterBuyerPurchases()/parseBuyerOrderQuery() — buyer Orders tab server-side search + active/history split, over PURCHASES so a page is 5 cards; the split reads the buyerAwaiting column, never `=== 'delivered'`
src/lib/pagination.ts           ← paginate()/parsePage() — generic page slicing, shared by all 6 admin list tabs (15/page) + seller Products/Orders/Messages (Products page size caller-chosen: 10/20/50/100)
src/lib/seller-products-query.ts ← filterAndSortSellerProducts/parseSellerProductQuery — Products tab search/sort(incl. purchased count)/category-filter + stock-status filter (stockBucket out/low/ok via LOW_STOCK_THRESHOLD, pstock)
src/lib/seller-orders-query.ts  ← filterAndSortSellerOrders/parseSellerOrderQuery — Orders tab search/sort/status-filter, scoped to one store's subtotal. **The tab runs on SQL now (`orders.ts#getSellerOrdersPage`) and this stays as the DEFINITION**, pinned by `tests/seller-orders-page-parity.test.ts`. URGENCY_GROUPS/_STATUSES/_RANKS are derived from the status table (never re-listed — money-guards refuses a second copy) and feed both sorts; the search term is capped like the journal's. Also ORDER_FILTER_STATUSES (what the filter menu can express — 'ready' omitted until shipping is wired) + ORDER_ACTIVE_STATUSES (the default view), the ONE source the SSR page, the API and the client toolbar all read; a second copy in the client had already drifted
src/lib/seller-messages-query.ts ← filterAndSortSellerMessages/buildSellerMessageRows/parseSellerMessageQuery — Messages tab search/sort/filter (pinned "הודעות מערכת" row excluded)
src/lib/seller-balance.ts       ← buildSellerBalances() — what each seller has EARNED (gross − commission at their OWN tier, per store then summed). REPORTING ONLY: the processor pays sellers directly, so nothing is ever marked paid. DERIVED, never stored — a table would be a second home for the same fact and would drift on the first cancellation/refund/slug change — and it costs no query, being built from the revenue map the admin cards already hold. NOT net of the monthly fee (billed to the card, not withheld). Shown as the fifth tile on the admin seller card
src/lib/seller-reports.ts       ← buildSalesReport()/buildProductSalesReport()/buildStockReport()/buildPayoutsReport() — the reports tab's four row sets, PURE (orders+products in, rows+totals out) so their invariants are assertable without a DB. Re-implements no rule: countsAsRevenue, orderNetForStore, businessDayISO, pricing.ts#commissionOnAgorot. A non-counting order is LISTED and excluded from the totals, never dropped — a month is reconciled against what happened. The product report allocates the ORDER's one discount across its lines (money.ts#allocateAgorot) so its revenue column equals the sales report's to the agora
src/lib/seller-report-shapes.ts ← ReportId/REPORT_IDS/isReportId/ACCOUNT_WIDE_REPORTS (which reports are NOT about the store in the switcher — it draws the picker's `כל החנויות` chip, so it is a declaration with a consumer rather than a comment)/LOW_STOCK_AT + the four row types, and NOTHING that reaches a database. Split out because the reports tab runs in the browser and seller-reports.ts imports orders.ts → db.ts, which opens a pool at module scope; tests/client-bundle-no-db.test.ts walks the import graph so this cannot be undone silently
src/lib/seller-reports-csv.ts   ← the four reports as a file Excel opens in Hebrew: BOM first, sanitizeCsvCell on every human-typed cell (a name starting `=` is a formula), CRLF, money as a bare decimal so it can be summed. The DATE stays ISO in the file while the screen shows DD/MM/YYYY — a spreadsheet parses ISO on any machine and renders it in the reader's locale, while `03/08/2026` is March 8th on a US one
src/lib/field-validity.ts       ← the site's replacement for the browser's validation bubble: fieldErrorMessage() maps a ValidityState to a sentence (empty first, then type, then bounds; `data-error` overrides all but required, setCustomValidity beats that), showFieldError/clearFieldError mark the field and keep aria-describedby's other ids intact. DOM half lives here because it is testable
src/scripts/form-validity.ts    ← wires that to the whole site from BaseLayout. Cancels the `invalid` EVENT (which kills the native bubble while the browser still blocks the submit) rather than setting novalidate — so a form written with novalidate opts itself out, late-rendered forms are covered, and a failed module load degrades to the native bubble. Focus moves to the first bad field in FORM order, which cancelling the event otherwise costs
src/pages/api/seller/reports.ts ← GET report=sales|products|stock, format=json|csv. Ownership from the session via getStoresBySellerId (never the slug), 731-day cap, no-store cache. `stock` ignores from/to on purpose: a stocktake is about now
src/scripts/dashboard/reports.ts ← the reports tab's client half: report picker, the SHARED range dropdown (date-range.ts presets + a custom from/to), the table, and client-side column sorting over the rows already loaded (a report is not paginated, so reordering cannot fail and never re-requests). Sorts by the underlying agorot, never the rendered cell. A new REPORT resets the sort, a new PERIOD keeps it. Filtering is deliberately absent — the export is the filtering story; the reasoning is above sortRows
src/components/dashboard/ReportsPanel.astro ← the tab's markup. Nothing server-rendered: every row arrives on first reveal, like Performance and Advertising. Under 700px each row becomes a labelled card (data-label + ::before in dashboard.css)
src/components/dashboard/PayoutsPanel.astro ← the Payments tab: two tiles (next payment · waiting), the bank block — a CLOSED masked summary with an ערוך button once an account is on file, the six inputs otherwise, both server-rendered with `hidden` so nothing flashes — the waiting-money split by reason, and #pay-how, the ONE place the hold rule is stated. Server-rendered (it is what pays for the tab badge); nothing on it pages any more
src/scripts/dashboard/payouts.ts ← that panel's only interactivity: one AJAX save. The server is the only validator — a refusal comes back naming the FIELD, which is what lets this focus the right box
src/lib/seller-performance.ts   ← buildPerformanceSummary()/pickGranularity() — revenue+orders+visits+unique-visitors aggregation (unique = union of daily visitor-id sets, per bucket + range total), day/month bucketed; takes commissionPercent → returns platformCommission/netProfit (reporting only); topLimit caps topProducts (default 5; <=0 = all, for breakdown modal). buildProductPerformance() — single-product drill-down (units/revenue from orders + views from product-pageviews.ts, same axis). buildZeroPoints() — empty-axis skeleton from dates alone (no I/O), for the platform aggregator
src/lib/platform-performance.ts ← buildPlatformPerformance() — app-wide aggregate for the admin ביצועים tab. PURE: sales and views are both pre-aggregated INPUTS (order-reporting.ts#getPlatformSales, store-pageviews.ts#getStoreViewStats), one query each for every store at once. It used to bucket every order on the platform once per store — O(stores × orders). buildPlatformSales is the JS twin, for tests. Plus selectStoreRows (search/sort/page over the breakdown rows)
src/lib/platform-ledger.ts      ← buildPlatformLedger() — PURE: the platform's balance sheet from the accrual + the payout plan. Sellers' money we hold (released + waiting), what leaves on the payout day, commission earned but not yet taken at source. Nothing floored at zero, deliberately: an overpayment is the state worth acting on, and flooring breaks the identity the card is read for
src/lib/platform-statement.ts   ← the monthly accounting statement's shapes + buildPlatformStatement() (pure) + monthPeriod/statementPeriod/recentMonthKeys. Two bases side by side, bridged by one figure; opening balance is the closing expression evaluated over everything BEFORE the period, which is what makes months chain. Its header says what is deliberately absent and why
src/lib/platform-statement-csv.ts ← that statement as a file Excel opens in Hebrew (BOM, CRLF, money as a plain decimal). Three columns — section, line, amount — so an accountant can filter the accrual rows away from the cash ones. No sanitizeCsvCell: every cell here is a label this file writes or a number we computed, and the moment a per-seller breakdown lands it needs one
src/lib/admin-statement-load.ts ← the statement's five reads as ONE wave, shared by the admin page and /api/admin/statement so the screen and the downloaded file cannot be two documents. Four of the five are the same two functions asked about the period and about everything before it
src/pages/api/admin/statement.ts ← the statement as JSON or CSV, admin cookie only. ?month=YYYY-MM or ?from=&to=; a range that IS a whole month resolves to that month, so the two spellings cannot describe one period differently
src/components/admin/AdminLedgerCard.astro ← the `כספים` card on the admin overview: what we hold that is not ours, split ready/waiting, the next transfer, and our uncollected commission
src/components/admin/AdminStatementPanel.astro ← the `דוח חשבונאי` tab: month or range picker, CSV download, print-to-PDF, and the document itself — two sections whose headings state their basis
src/scripts/admin/statement.ts  ← that tab's period controls. Every change is a server round trip through swapPanel, never a client recomputation: a second implementation of five aggregates in the browser is a second answer to what August closed at
src/lib/platform-revenue.ts     ← buildPlatformRevenue() — the platform's OWN income for a range, split three ways: sales commission (passed in from the summary, never recomputed) + subscriptions (each seller's tier fee, pro-rated by days since signup; free trial NOT modelled → upper bound) + margin on seller ad spend (spend itself is pass-through, not income). Pure — caller supplies the seller + campaign lists
src/components/dashboard/OnboardingChecklist.astro ← "first steps" card (Overview tab); hides itself when complete; required steps get a חובה chip + a "not discoverable" banner
src/components/dashboard/TabHint.astro ← one-line "what this tab does" explainer; parent gates visibility
src/components/dashboard/StaleDataBar.astro ← floating "another tab changed this store" notice + refresh/dismiss; hidden via the `!hidden` utility (the `hidden` ATTRIBUTE loses to display:flex), raised by tab-sync.ts. Sits 5.5rem up so UnsavedChangesBar can have the bottom slot; each bar positions ITSELF, because the elevation guard refuses to let an ancestor's `fixed` vouch for a shadow
src/components/dashboard/UnsavedChangesBar.astro ← floating "you have unsaved changes in <section>" notice, raised by unsaved-guard.ts. A SENTENCE, not a tab marker: a dot there was built and rejected 2026-08-05 (the messages tab already owns that corner dot, and a seller has no editors' convention to read it by). Its button goes to the section and focuses save — it never submits a form the seller cannot see
src/components/dashboard/CreateStoreCard.astro ← store-creation form, shared by the empty-account state and ?new=1 (additional store)
src/components/dashboard/PerfProductSection.astro ← per-product drill-down section (picker trigger + KPIs + charts); shared by seller dashboard + admin per-store performance page
src/components/dashboard/TopProductsList.astro ← the leading-products ROWS (not the box); the one Astro renderer for all three performance surfaces. `showStore` names the seller — platform list only. Client twin: `renderTopProducts` in scripts/dashboard/performance.ts; a third copy fails shared-ui-recipe-guards
src/lib/top-product-share.ts     ← what the % on that row is a share of: `productShare(revenue, productRevenueAgorot)` → `{pct,label}`, `<1%` rather than a 0% beside a real figure, and 0 for a non-finite total (a new client reading an old API response mid-deploy)
src/components/dashboard/InfoTip.astro ← shared "(i)" info-icon → floating tooltip (data-tooltip + initInfoTooltips); used across the admin advertising panel
src/components/dashboard/CampaignHistory.astro ← the collapsed, button-free "קמפיינים קודמים" block (finished + cancelled), on the seller tab and the admin twin; filled by the same client renderer from the same GET, first time it is opened
src/components/dashboard/BoostFormFields.astro ← every field of the "launch a boost" form, shared by the seller Advertising tab and the admin per-store twin (each page keeps only its own <form>, notes and submit). One component because both are driven by the SAME script, which finds fields by id — a drifted copy fails silently, and they had already drifted once
src/components/dashboard/DashTabsBoot.astro ← INLINE (is:inline) tab controller: switches panels, records+replays an [aria-haspopup] click made in a panel still [data-hydrating], and re-fires dashtab:show on DOMContentLoaded for a tab opened pre-hydration. Every page with a `.dash-tabs` strip must render it (tests/dash-tabs-boot.test.ts) — initDashTabs() only adds keyboard/overflow and calls window.__dashTabActivate
src/components/dashboard/SkelBar.astro ← the one loading placeholder for a fetched value; caller passes Tailwind sizing so the bar matches the value it replaces. Geometry lives ONCE in seller/dashboard.astro's SKEL map, read back off `data-skel` by performance.ts on every range switch
src/components/dashboard/SellerMessageRow.astro ← one Messages-tab thread (main <tr> + collapsible thread <tr>); buyer AND system threads render through it — row.kind only picks the delete button + reply placeholder. messages.ts's buildMessageRow() must mirror this markup exactly (AJAX pages)
src/components/dashboard/PromotionsPanel.astro ← seller "מבצעים" tab markup (store-sale form + on-sale roll-up); behaviour lives in scripts/dashboard/promotions.ts
src/components/dashboard/CouponsCard.astro ← third card in that tab: the coupon editor shell + an empty list the client fills. One form for create AND edit; only code+value required, everything else optional
src/components/dashboard/CopyButton.astro ← copy button for an address the seller has to paste elsewhere; copies its target's LIVE text
src/components/dashboard/FormFallbackGuard.astro ← is:inline guard, and the dashboard's whole local draft-recovery: blocks the browser's native submit when no AJAX handler claimed it, drafts every `data-unsaved-guard` form to localStorage while it is edited (debounced; `data-draft-live` limits that to forms a later load can find again) and OFFERS it back next load — only the fields that differ from the `base` snapshot the page rendered with, so an untouched field never reverts another tab (never the `_csrf` field — SKIP_NAME; a draft written before a sign-in carries a token bound to the other identity, and restoring it over the fresh one is a 403 nobody can explain). **The offer is shown in TWO places and they are ONE offer (2026-08-07, owner):** the bar inside the form, and `#dash-draft-bar` floating at the bottom whenever that bar is not in front of him — measured BELOW the pinned chrome, since a bar behind the fixed header is as unreadable as one in a closed tab. **What the floating one offers is decided by WHERE THE FORM IS, not by which bar is showing.** Same tab (he scrolled past it): it IS that bar, word for word — same sentence, same שחזר/מחק, the same `acceptOffer`/`rejectOffer` behind them, and no section name, which would name where he already is. Another tab: it names the section and LEADS him there (`unsavedGo`), and the bar he can now see does the asking — nothing answers for a form he cannot see. One offer at a time: a button that ACTS needs an unambiguous subject, which is why there is no "in several places" wording. Restoring scrolls the form into view ONLY when the bar he pressed was off screen. It sits at `bottom-6` and steps to `bottom-[9.5rem]` only while UnsavedChangesBar or StaleDataBar is out: **a rare-collision slot must not be paid for by the common case.** **`touched` is why a restore cannot overwrite him:** the offer's payload used to be frozen at load, so typing into an offered field and pressing restore wrote the older value on top — newest wins per field now, and `save` writes the UNION (on-screen values + offered values for untouched fields) so his new typing cannot evict the work the bar is still offering. A form that really wants a browser POST needs data-native-submit — INCLUDING one the global chrome renders on top of the dashboard, since the listener is on `document`: Footer's /api/lang toggle was blocked this way and told the seller saving was unavailable (2026-07-31, tests/global-chrome-forms.test.ts)
src/lib/store-hours.ts          ← DEFAULT_STORE_HOURS + parseStoreHoursForm()/formatStoreHours() (day-label/time rows for the store details popover) + formatStoreHoursSchema() (LocalBusiness JSON-LD openingHours) + googleMapsSearchUrl()
src/lib/bot-detect.ts           ← isBotUserAgent/isBotRequest — the ONE gate keeping crawlers, link previewers, monitors and headless browsers out of every view counter and funnel event (middleware + /api/store-product). UA-only by design; biased toward false positives
src/lib/store-pageviews.ts      ← **Postgres since 2026-08-02** (`store_page_views` + `store_page_view_visitors`). recordPageView(storeId,visitorId?) is a single-intent call into `page-view-tap.ts`, which owns the statement; getStoreViewStats takes MANY store ids at once (the platform aggregation asks for all 45 in one query) and getViewStatsForStore wraps the single case. Keyed by store ID, not slug — a store renaming its URL keeps its history with nothing to migrate. `totalUniqueVisitors` is its OWN COUNT(DISTINCT) over the range and is deliberately not derivable from the buckets: summing per-bucket uniques double-counts everyone who came back. **getPlatformViewStats is the same question for a SET of stores read as one audience** — the admin tab used to sum per-store uniques, which counts a cross-store shopper once per store (60 reported where 37 people had been), inflating exactly as the mall starts working and deflating the conversion rate that divides by it. Per-store uniques cannot be de-duped by an aggregator that holds counts and not ids, so this is a second query, not arithmetic
src/lib/visitor-retention.ts     ← VISITOR_RETENTION_DAYS (400) + visitorRetentionCutoffISO() — the ONE definition of how far back per-visitor detail is kept, read by the purge job AND by the admin note that promises it, so the screen cannot outlive the window. Cutoff comes from the business calendar, never CURRENT_DATE (the server is UTC, the platform day is Asia/Jerusalem). Only `analytics_visitors` and `store_page_view_visitors` are bounded; the aggregate counters are kept forever, which is why an old range still reports views in full and uniques as 0
src/lib/chart-svg.ts            ← buildBarChartSvg()/buildLineChartSvg()/buildMultiLineChartSvg()/buildDonutChartSvg() — isomorphic inline-SVG charts (SSR + client re-render); BarChartPoint has optional `tipLabel` (tooltip override for a truncated axis label) + `color` (per-bar fill, e.g. boost tint); multi-line overlays N x-aligned series (dashed secondary must skip pathLength or its dasharray renders solid); shared y-axis/gutter helpers; `rtl` opt flips y-axis side; `preserveAspectRatio="none"` fills container; lines are monotone-cubic curves (`smoothPathD` — no overshoot below the baseline); height is pinned as an inline style, never left to the `height` attribute (reset.css `svg{height:auto}` wins over it). **A LINE chart answers only on its marks:** the point's full-height column rect is `pointer-events="none"` and only carries the tooltip text, while hovering happens on a per-point `.chart-hit` circle (r=9 against the drawn 2.4) or a `.chart-hit-line` — an invisible 14px stroke tracing the same curve, `pointer-events="stroke"` with no fill so the area under it stays inert. Bars are untouched; there the bar IS the mark
src/lib/ad-campaigns.ts         ← **Postgres since 2026-08-02** (`ad_campaigns`); every accessor is async and the budget is `monthlyBudgetAgorot` (integer agorot — the seller still types shekels, converted in ad-campaign-input.ts). AdCampaign CRUD (platform google|meta|both, scope store|product|products|categories, +durationDays/audience{gender,age_group}, +pausedAt for freeze-on-pause, +pausedReason 'unavailable'|'out-of-stock' cleared on resume, +archivedAt) + getCampaignsByStoreId (live) / getArchivedByStoreId (history) / archiveCampaign — there is deliberately NO delete: every reported figure is summed from these rows, so removing one rewrote a past month's ad spend. updateCampaign refuses an archived row via `WHERE archived_at IS NULL` in the statement (history is read-only, and a predicate cannot be raced) and is scoped by store_id (an id is not a permission); updateCampaigns/archiveCampaigns/archiveCampaignsForStore are the batch forms the health sweep and store closure use instead of a write per campaign + parseDuration/parseAudience (untrusted-input coercion)
src/lib/ad-baseline.ts          ← storeBaselineStatus() — is this store actually IN the platform-funded feed, and what did it accrue over the 30 days its label names? Pure; a store with no visible product (store-readiness.ts) or an admin block gets active:false + 0, because "active" on a store the feed doesn't carry is the one claim on that card a seller could act on and be wrong about
src/lib/ad-campaign-input.ts    ← buildCampaignInput() — the ONE validation path turning an untrusted boost POST into a CreateCampaignInput, shared by the seller route and its admin twin: re-checks product/category ownership against the store, caps 50 products / 8 categories, collapses a one-product pick back to the flat single-product shape. `idList` is Set-deduped and stops one past the cap — it was `includes` over a request-controlled array, i.e. quadratic (40k ids = ~5s of blocked SSR)
src/lib/ad-budget.ts            ← MIN/MAX_CAMPAIGN_BUDGET + AD_BUDGET_PRESETS + isValidCampaignBudget() — pure (browser-importable) so the create form's min/max, the inline editor and BOTH routes' POST+PATCH read one rule. There was no ceiling and each PATCH re-typed its own floor, so `1e12` was a valid budget and "create at the floor, patch under it" worked
src/lib/ad-campaign-health.ts   ← campaignHealth()/getCampaignsForStore()/getCampaignHistory()/resumeBlockReason() — is a boost still advertising anything reachable? `advertisable` (2026-08-06) is a THIRD count beside live/buyable: a product with no photo is on the shelf and absent from Merchant Center, and the card used to count it. Deliberately NOT folded into `live` — that is the starved state, which pauses as 'unavailable' and waits for a human, and a missing photo fixes itself the moment one is uploaded. So it informs and pauses nothing; it now also PAUSES, on its own third stored reason (owner's call 2026-08-06, migration 0016) WHO STOPPED IT DECIDES WHO RESUMES: off the storefront (hidden/blocked/deleted product, or an admin-blocked STORE — then no product is reachable at all) → paused 'unavailable', only a human resumes; a showcase store, or a store whose whole catalogue the ad networks PROHIBIT → also 'unavailable' (2026-08-06/07): `lib/ad-policy.ts` is what we may not SUBMIT to Google/Meta, which is a different question from what a seller may SELL — both networks suspend the ACCOUNT for prohibited content and the platform advertises everyone through one, so it is excluded from the FEED while the product keeps selling. The list is narrow ON PURPOSE (a wrongly-excluded product is a silent harm the seller pays for) — "אקדח" is also a staple/nail/glue/heat gun, and 13 such products are pinned as MUST-NOT-match. A text list cannot catch a prohibited product described innocuously; the network's own item verdict is what sees that, and wiring `merchant-status` disapprovals into an actual exclusion is GO_LIVE §2. Everything sold out → paused 'out-of-stock' and the SAME sweep resumes it when stock returns; NOTHING in it has a photo → paused 'no-image' (2026-08-06, migration 0016), self-healing the same way, and a third value rather than a reuse because "אזל מהמלאי" on a card about a photo is a false statement to the seller; ran its fixed duration → archived into history on its own. The stored reason always names the blocker that is true NOW: the two self-healing reasons re-stamp between each other and both up to 'unavailable' if the product leaves the shelf, never the reverse — the reason is a promise the card makes about who has to act. `resumeBlockCode` is the ONE reason→error-code mapping (it was duplicated across the seller and admin routes, so a new reason had to be added twice or one route would tell the seller a human must intervene on something self-healing); its guard test reads the client and both locales. Partly gone/sold out keeps running and the card says how many. The sweep runs on the READ rather than being hooked into every product mutation — one idempotent call site instead of a list that rots; becomes a query predicate at the DB migration
src/lib/ad-scope-label.ts       ← AdScopeKind + campaignScopeName()/campaignTargetingLabel()/campaignHealthNote() — pure/isomorphic wording for a campaign card, shared by the seller SSR, the admin twin and the client rebuild in advertising.ts (three copies before, which is how they drifted)
src/lib/ad-metrics.ts           ← campaignStatsInRange()/campaignLifetimeStats()/campaignRunPeriod()/withCampaignStats()/brandStatsInRange()/baselineImpressionsInRange() — range-aware+lifetime seeded MOCK ad metrics (O(1) per entity, node:crypto seed); `runPeriod` freezes accrued metrics at pause (pausedAt) + caps by duration; 'both' platform = blended CPM `spendOver()` is pacing × delivery: a fixed-duration budget paces over ITS period (not 30), and a seeded 82–98% utilisation keeps the cap a ceiling rather than a guaranteed charge — separate seed from CPM/CTR, which are independent of it in reality.
src/lib/date-range.ts           ← presetRange()/daysInRangeInclusive()/previousPeriod()/coerceRange()/toISODate()/shortDate()/resolveAdRange() — pure isomorphic date-range helpers for the ad range-picker (presets today/7d/30d/thisMonth/custom/lifetime); resolveAdRange = shared window-vs-lifetime resolver for both ad routes. Also QUICK_RANGE_PRESETS/quickRange() — the TOOLBAR vocabulary (היום/השבוע/החודש/30 יום), separate because a journal has no 'lifetime'; every bound derives from one business-day string. **PERIOD_PRESETS/periodRange()/PERIOD_PRESET_LABEL_KEY (2026-08-10)** — the seven-preset family the PERIOD surfaces use (adds lastMonth, which a bookkeeper needs and quickRange has no name for); promoted out of performance.ts's private closure when reports became a third copy, because two dashboard tabs saying 'החודש שעבר' had to mean the same days. displayDate() — an ISO day as DD/MM/YYYY for a person, by string surgery: `new Date(iso)` gives it a zone it does not have and can print the day before
src/lib/checkout-group.ts       ← checkoutGroupKey() + CHECKOUT_GROUP_KEY_SQL — what makes N order rows ONE purchase, in both languages because the admin page slice is a query. tests/checkout-group-key.test.ts requires the two spellings to agree
src/lib/toolbar-range-picker.ts ← openRangePicker() — the date-window popover shared by the money journal and the Alerts tab. Knows nothing about either tab's query params; hands back {from,to}
src/lib/admin-ads.ts            ← buildPlatformAdOverview() — platform advertising overview (pure, range-aware): exposure total + by-source + top-N stores (capped, scalability) + PPC (own ads only) + brand/boost aggregates + promoted stores
src/lib/brand-campaigns.ts      ← **Postgres since 2026-08-02** (`brand_campaigns`), moved in the same diff as its boost twin on purpose (memory project_brand_boost_twin_drift). BrandCampaign CRUD + parseCreateInput/parseBrandBudgetAgorot(MAX_BRAND_BUDGET)/sanitizeDestination(scheme-guard)/sanitizeImageUrl(https-only) — owner-created platform-brand ads (creative: headline/body/image/destination); the mock stats moved to ad-metrics.ts
src/lib/platform-ads.ts         ← getPlatformAdSettings()/updatePlatformAdSettings()/parseLifetimeBudgetAgorot — owner's baseline campaign knobs (`lifetimeBudgetAgorot` + active/paused), stored **since 2026-08-02 in `app_settings['platform_ads']`**, a jsonb row. A jsonb value takes no column type and no CHECK, so `coerce()` IS the constraint and runs on every read as well as every write
src/lib/ad-tier.ts              ← roasTier()/ctrTier() + roasTierChipHtml()/ctrTierChipHtml() — pure/isomorphic נמוך/בינוני/גבוה benchmark chip for the two quality metrics (client-safe, no node deps)
src/lib/order-age.ts            ← orderAgeChipHtml() — pure/isomorphic order-age pill for the Orders tab; pending escalates calm→amber→red by age, in-progress shows calm age, delivered = none. No card border, no admin pulse (both dropped 2026-07-23 — the chip alone signals lateness). The chip span is `shrink-0` on purpose: the card's slot clips it, so without that the squeeze lands on the 11px clock SVG
src/lib/order-notify.ts         ← notifyOrderStatusChanged() — source-agnostic pipeline: on a real status change fires the in-app notification (registered buyers) + the status email (ALL buyers incl. guests). buildOrderStatusNotification is pure/tested
src/lib/order-status-copy.ts    ← STATUS_MESSAGES — single copy source (ready/shipped/cancelled) shared by the in-app + email channels so wording can't drift
src/lib/store-status.ts         ← the five store states as one rules table (pure) + storeLifecycle/isStoreReachable/isStoreDiscoverable/canStoreSell/storeHttpStatus/showsPausedNotice
src/lib/store-lifecycle.ts      ← the only writer of those flags: pauseStore/resumeStore/requestStoreClosure/settleStoreClosure + openOrderCount/countOpenOrdersByStore
src/lib/store-state-badge.ts    ← admin-facing label+colour per state, one map for the .astro badge and the JS-rendered table
src/lib/sheet-drag.ts           ← drag a bottom sheet down to dismiss (touch only); used by CartDrawer on mobile
src/pages/store-unavailable.astro ← paused store's page (200, notice only, rewritten to from the storefront)
src/pages/store-gone.astro      ← closed store's page (410 — its own route because Astro forces 404 on a rewrite to /404)
src/pages/api/seller/store-lifecycle.ts ← seller pause/resume/close endpoint
src/pages/api/seller/coupons.ts ← the seller's coupon collection (GET list, POST save/delete); ownership from the session's own store list on every verb, and store-coupons.ts scopes each statement by store_id as well
src/pages/api/seller/payout-details.ts ← POST the bank + business fields of the SESSION's account (no storeId anywhere — a payout is per registered business). parsePayoutDetails validates, updateSellerPayoutDetails writes with its own statement so a clear reaches the column as NULL, and a moved account is announced to the seller — in a notification that names NO part of the account, deliberately (a notification is read where the dashboard is not, and will be an email once GO_LIVE §4 lands); tests/payout-details.test.ts asserts the digits never come back
src/pages/api/cart/coupon.ts ← the buyer's "is this code real" lookup: TERMS only (no money figure — the page computes it with the same pure function), one `unknown` for every miss so it cannot confirm a guess, throttled per store per address
src/scripts/dashboard/store-lifecycle.ts ← the settings-tab control for it
src/scripts/dashboard/coupons.ts ← initCouponsCard() — list/create/edit/delete over /api/seller/coupons, list redrawn from the response; the row's status word comes from the server's `live` flag, never a second date comparison here
src/scripts/checkout-coupon.ts ← the buyer's coupon disclosure: markup as a string (the cart is rebuilt on every render), applied-code state across renders, and couponSavingIls() through the same pure resolver /api/checkout charges by
src/components/admin/AdminStoreStateBadge.astro ← state badge for every admin store list
src/lib/email/                  ← transactional email module: index.ts (sendEmail() + env-driven adapter select) · adapter.ts (EmailAdapter contract) · resend-adapter.ts / console-adapter.ts · template.ts (branded RTL shell) · parts.ts (shared pure render helpers) · order-emails.ts (confirmation: buyer+seller) · order-confirmation.ts (checkout orchestrator, resolves recipients) · order-status-email.ts (ready/shipped/cancelled) · store-lifecycle-email.ts (seller: paused/closing/closed/reopened — HTML and text render from one copy object)
src/lib/audience-infer.ts       ← inferAudienceGender()/inferAgeGroup() — pure keyword inference of a product's target gender + age_group from category path/name/tags (he+en, collision-safe regexes); drives campaign auto-fill + the feed
src/lib/merchant-status.ts      ← the contract + provider registry for asking Google/Meta what they did with our feed. `fetchStatuses()` returns null for ANY failure to reach an answer — network, HTTP, unmintable token, and a 200 whose body is not the shape expected — because a wrong field name parsing as "zero rejected items" would report health forever, which is the silent rejection it exists to catch, one layer up. No stub: with no credentials the registry is empty and the job is inert. clampIssue/clampIssueCode cap the third-party text before it reaches a notification body and a related_id, neither of which clamps anything
src/lib/merchant-status-google.ts ← Content API v2.1 adapter. TWO endpoints because Google reports two kinds of failure: datafeedstatuses (did the FEED get in — one illegal character drops every store at once, and item statuses would then describe the last GOOD ingest) and productstatuses. A feed error ENDS the pass. `productId` is Google's composite REST id — offerIdFromGoogleProductId splits from the RIGHT, since our ids may contain a colon inside a Hebrew variant value
src/lib/merchant-status-meta.ts  ← Graph API catalog adapter. Deliberately NO separate feed-level call: Meta's equivalent could not be verified against a live account and a guess would either invent a silence or raise a permanent false alarm — a total failure already shows as an empty catalogue, which is treated as bad news. `pending` counts as serving (every new product passes through it; alerting there fires on ordinary product creation)
src/lib/merchant-status-check.ts ← the pass: alert routing, the rejection ceiling, seller notifications, and the "have I already said this" windows. Severity is `error`, not `critical`, by error-severity.ts's own rule — a dead feed costs marketing, not a purchase, and nothing is left inconsistent. Above rejectionCeiling(n) = max(20, n/4) the per-seller notifications are HELD and one platform alert is raised instead: a rate that high is our feed, our account or a policy change, and blaming 200 sellers is not recoverable an hour later
src/lib/google-auth.ts          ← service-account JWT → access token, cached per client_email+scope. Minted in-process, never pasted into an env var: Google's tokens expire hourly, so a variable someone refreshes every hour is the manual step this deletes — failing in the worst shape, since the monitor that watches for silent failures would start failing silently itself
src/lib/product-feed.ts         ← buildProductFeedAttributes() (per-product Merchant/Catalog attrs; brand = the product's own field, falling back to the store name; empty description falls back to title, title/description clamped 150/5000) + buildFeedItems() (variant→item_group_id expansion — and each combo row carries its OWN image (variantImages, falling back to images[0]), its OWN landing (variant-landing.ts) and its OWN availability label; all three were derived once and spread onto every row until 2026-08-06, so a `color=אדום` row shipped the blue photo and custom_label_2 reported the shared pool while the availability ATTRIBUTE reported the combo's bucket; images absolutised, a product ships if ANY survives; `link` comes from ctx.productLink = custom-domain.ts#productCanonicalUrl, so the feed and the page's canonical are ONE value even on a seller's own domain; every attribute clamped to the published Merchant limit, an over-length identifier dropped rather than cut; sale_price carries sale_price_effective_date whenever the winning discount has an end date — the feed is FETCHED on Google's schedule, so without it an expired sale keeps being advertised until the next crawl, and the times are the Israeli day written out because Google's default is UTC) + isProductAdvertisable() (the feed's own exclusion rule — usable image + real price — exported so ad-campaign-health stops counting products the feed silently drops; NOT the question merchant-status asks, which is what the NETWORKS did with a row we sent) + toMerchantXml() (RSS 2.0 g:-namespace serializer; xmlText strips XML-illegal chars — one would break the WHOLE feed) — pure. Split into feedXmlHeader/feedItemXml/FEED_XML_FOOTER so the document can be STREAMED part by part (feed-document.ts) with toMerchantXml composed of exactly those pieces
src/lib/product-labels.ts       ← deriveProductLabels() — 5 stable positional custom_label slots (price/performance/availability/audience/store-type), all zero-touch/auto-derived; LABEL_SLOTS = the positional contract; exports performanceTier()/PERFORMANCE_TIERS (shared with product-listing.ts' storefront ranking) + availabilityTier()/AVAILABILITY_SLOT (the ONE slot that is not a property of the product as a whole — a variant row recomputes it from its own bucket); feeds product-feed.ts (tests/product-labels.test.ts)
src/lib/tag-suggest.ts          ← search-TAG helpers (distinct from custom_labels): suggestTags() (name/desc words + curated attribute lexicon, for the click-to-add row) + deriveAutoTags() (category-path + variant values only — the high-confidence subset /api/product auto-applies on save). Pure. Tags feed site/product search (name+tags, NOT description). AI tag extraction was explored+removed — real engine deferred to DB/semantic search, see memory project_ai_tagging_deferred (tests/tag-suggest.test.ts)
src/lib/indexnow.ts             ← pingIndexNow()/pingProductChange()/pingStoreChange() — best-effort IndexNow submit to Bing (→ChatGPT/Copilot) on product/store change; no-op until seo.indexNowKey + real domain; pure indexNowEnabled/buildIndexNowPayload tested. Key file served at src/pages/[key].txt.ts; AI-engine site map at src/pages/llms.txt.ts
src/lib/sitemap.ts              ← buildUrlSetXml()/toSitemapDate() — pure sitemap <urlset> serialiser; re-exports xmlEscape from xml-text.ts. Split into URLSET_XML_HEADER/urlEntryXml/URLSET_XML_FOOTER so the platform copy can be STREAMED part by part and the one-shot version stays composed of exactly those pieces. Also buildSitemapIndexXml() + SITEMAP_MAX_URLS (45,000 — the spec's ceiling is 50,000 per FILE and it is rejected whole above that, verified at sitemaps.org 2026-08-09; the margin is because the count moves with every product a seller adds)
src/lib/artifacts.ts            ← pre-built public documents: ArtifactWriter.open/write/close (one walk can produce SEVERAL documents — the sitemap's shard boundaries are not known until the walk reaches them, and re-walking per shard would multiply the only expensive part) + writeArtifact(name, chunks, detail) on top of it + pruneArtifacts(prefix, keep) (a shard the new build did not write must be DELETED, or it keeps serving a slice of a catalogue that no longer exists). Flushes every ~256KB (memory bound AND an await that hands the event loop back), publishes by moving live_generation in one transaction, keeps the PREVIOUS generation so a reader mid-stream is never truncated; artifactStream reads READ_WINDOW=8 parts per query (measured: a part per query served 2.5MB in 3.3s, a window of 8 in 2.1–2.5s, and the rest is the document crossing the network from Neon); artifactResponse adds ETag=generation → 304, Last-Modified, Content-Length; artifactUnavailable = 503+Retry-After, never an empty 200 (an empty feed says every product is gone). Migration 0022
src/lib/catalog-artifacts.ts    ← the documents by name (FEED_ARTIFACT, SITEMAP_ARTIFACT = the sitemap INDEX, sitemapShardArtifact(n)/sitemapShardUrl(n) = the same fact said once), CATALOG_ARTIFACT_INTERVAL_SEC (30m — deliberately BOTH the rebuild interval and the routes' max-age, so worst-case staleness stays the hour it was; why no "rebuild only if changed" fingerprint is argued there), rebuildCatalogArtifact()/rebuildSitemapArtifacts() for the jobs, serveCatalogArtifact()/serveSitemapShard() for the routes. **The index is the LAST write and shards are pruned only after it**, so a crash mid-rebuild leaves the previous sitemap whole. A name never built answers 503 and starts the build BEHIND the response under singleFlight — never for the caller; an out-of-range SHARD is 404 instead, because it is not coming
src/lib/catalog-build.ts        ← STORE_BATCH (20 stores per round trip — the memory ceiling of a build and the rhythm the event loop gets back), CatalogBuildStats, batches(). Shared by the two document builders, which never import each other
src/lib/feed-document.ts        ← the product feed as an async generator: feedChannelMeta(), storeFeedItems() (the whole per-store policy, exported so the equality test composes its expectation from it rather than a second copy) and feedDocumentChunks(stats), which yields per item with the separator join('\n') used to add. Concatenated = exactly toMerchantXml() over the same items
src/lib/sitemap-document.ts     ← storeEntries() — the ONE definition of a store's crawlable surface, used by the platform build AND by the route's custom-domain branch — plus platformStoreEntries() (null = on its own domain, or not ready) and platformSitemapEntries(stats), which yields ENTRIES rather than XML because this document is sharded and the writer has to count URLs, not newlines
src/lib/xml-text.ts             ← xmlText()/xmlEscape()/xmlCdata() — the ONE XML escaper (2026-08-02). product-feed.ts and sitemap.ts each had a private `xmlEscape`; only the feed's stripped XML-illegal control chars, so the sitemap's was silently weaker on the document Google parses. An illegal char makes the WHOLE document unparseable, not one entry — `tests/xml-text.test.ts` fails if either file grows a copy back
src/lib/error-log.ts            ← ErrorLogEntry (+storeSlug/storeName/actorRole/actorId/actorLabel/resolutionHint/severity), logError()/getRecentErrors()/clearErrorLog()/truncateStack(). Identity comes from request-actor.ts, not from here
src/lib/request-actor.ts        ← resolveRequestActor(pathname,cookies) — THE definition of "who was this request and which store was it about": first path segment → store (percent-decoded, slugs carry Hebrew), session → seller iff it owns a store. Extracted 2026-08-13 because error-log.ts and user-reports.ts render one above the other on the Alerts tab; tests/request-actor.test.ts greps src/ so a third copy fails
src/lib/user-reports.ts         ← visitor "דווח על תקלה" reports: createUserReport/getRecentReports/countOpenReports/setReportHandled. Body supplies kind+message+email only; page/store/role/id are server-derived. page_url goes through safe-redirect.ts (the admin renders it as a link)
src/lib/image-moderation.ts     ← the verdict half of image moderation, pure. `moderationRefusal` refuses ONLY rejected/aborted — pending/queued go through, because Cloudinary's docs leave open whether the verdict is even in the upload response, and under the async reading refusing pending would refuse every photo the day the add-on is switched on. `moderationWentMissing(response, expected)` is the other half: an add-on that hit its quota STOPS, and its response is identical to a never-enabled one's, so the only thing that can tell them apart is our own `PUBLIC_IMAGE_MODERATION_ON` — compared per upload, reported to the Alerts tab, never blocking. **A log row says something HAPPENED; "a protection is not running" is a STATE**, so the answer is also a section in WORDS (AdminModerationStatus.astro + image-moderation-health.ts), and it renders in two places answering two questions: the **Overview always** ("is the filter on?" is a standing condition, and nothing else in the product answers it) and the **Alerts tab only when something is wrong** (that tab is defined by problems — a green "all fine" line there is the furniture that teaches an eye to skip where the real alarm appears). Owner, 2026-08-13, over three rejected placements: in the log alone it is buried at the quietest severity; as a stat card on the Overview grid it reads as a metric; and as a permanent section in Alerts it had no business being in a tab called "התראות ושגיאות" while healthy. A signal has to be shaped like what it says AND live where that question is asked. GO_LIVE §2.6 is the owner step
src/lib/image-moderation-health.ts ← getImageModerationState() — 'ok'/'off'/'stopped', read by BOTH admin tabs (Overview always, Alerts only when not ok). 'off' needs no query; 'stopped' asks the error log whether MODERATION_MISSING_MARKER has been reported in the last 21 days, and the window is what lets the card clear itself. Matching our OWN constant is not the message-classification error-severity.ts refuses — the marker is pinned by tests/image-moderation.test.ts
src/lib/error-severity.ts       ← deriveSeverity()/isMoneyPath()/SEVERITY_ORDER — critical|error|warning, from source+route+status ONLY (never the message: request text must not choose how loudly it is reported). Every client report is a warning — volume distinguishes a real outage, and one entry has none. Stored by logError, never accepted from the caller
src/lib/error-reference.ts      ← errorRef() (#8-char code linking the alert mail to the dashboard row — NOT seq, which the DB assigns after the mail is built), errorMeaning() (plain-Hebrew "what happened to a person"), errorCopyText() (the ONE paste block; the mail and the row's copy button had drifted apart)
src/lib/critical-alert.ts       ← alertOnCriticalError() — the only thing that reaches for a person. Off unless ALERT_EMAIL. Every knob is a limit: 1 mail/route/15min, 10/hour, a failed dedup query SUPPRESSES. Never calls logError (it runs inside it)
src/lib/stream-errors.ts        ← reportStreamErrors() — the half of every page that renders AFTER `await next()` resolved, which middleware's try/catch structurally cannot see (every component's frontmatter, BaseLayout included). Reports and re-raises; cannot recover, the headers are gone
src/lib/process-errors.ts       ← ensureProcessErrorHandlersInstalled() — a rejected promise with no request behind it. Re-throws to PRESERVE Node's default crash; installing a listener would otherwise suppress it. No uncaughtException handler on purpose
src/lib/outbound-fetch.ts       ← outboundFetch() — the one way this app calls a server that is not itself. Node's fetch waits 300s for headers, so a hung provider parked the buyer's request behind it. 10s default; tests/outbound-fetch-guard.test.ts fails a bare fetch() to an absolute URL. Runs in a BROWSER bundle too, and must — a dashboard fetch of a stored Cloudinary original left the seller's button spinning forever; the guard test cannot see that shape, so the rule is in the module header
src/lib/single-flight.ts        ← singleFlight(key, build) — concurrent callers for one expensive build share it. A de-duplicator, NOT a cache: nobody is served a finished snapshot, so freshness is unchanged. Since 2026-08-09 its one caller is the COLD build in catalog-artifacts.ts (a database that has never held the document); the two routes it was written for no longer build anything
src/lib/format-date.ts          ← formatHeDateTime() — isomorphic (server frontmatter + client scripts). Plus formatBusinessTime/formatBusinessDateShort/formatBusinessDayLabel, which render in BUSINESS_TIMEZONE — use those wherever a timestamp sits beside a figure bucketed by businessDayISO, or a 00:30 Israel event prints yesterday's date under today's heading
src/lib/html-escape.ts          ← escapeHtml() — THE HTML escaper, isomorphic (pure string, no DOM: SSR + client + email). Escapes &<>"' so it's correct in a text node AND in an attribute. 20 hand-written local copies were folded into it 2026-07-29; call sites keep their old name via an import alias (esc/escH/escHtml/escB/escEom/escMsg/pqvEsc). Guarded by tests/html-escape.test.ts
src/lib/json-script.ts          ← jsonForScript() — the ONLY way to put data in a <script> (set:html). JSON.stringify alone is an XSS sink: `</script>` is valid JSON and closes the tag. Escapes <>& + U+2028/2029 to \uXXXX (still valid JSON, JSON.parse decodes it). Guarded by tests/json-script.test.ts
src/lib/image-url.ts            ← sanitizeImageUrl()/sanitizeImageUrls() — server-side validation for every image URL arriving in a request: https:// or site-relative only, and the stored value is the URL parser's own re-serialization (quotes/spaces percent-encoded). Used by product-form.ts, /api/store.ts, brand-campaigns.ts. toAbsoluteImageUrl(raw,baseUrl) is the FEED form — a site-relative path is valid storage but an invalid image_link
src/lib/http-compress.ts        ← gzipResponse()/shouldCompress() — gzips every SSR text response (HTML/JSON/XML), streamed not buffered; skips already-encoded, non-text and bodyless ones, drops content-length, adds Vary. Homepage: 274KB → 42KB on the wire, HTML delivery 3.5s → 0.6s at 1.6Mbps. @astrojs/node has no compression and serves dist/client with `send`, which can't gzip and won't serve precompressed .gz — so static CSS/JS/fonts still need gzip/brotli at the proxy/CDN (GO_LIVE §1). Guarded by tests/http-compress.test.ts
src/middleware.ts               ← onRequest — custom-domain routing (active Host→store from root; 404s an unclaimed external host) + root-level store/product view + analytics taps; logs only thrown/uncaught server errors, never a route's deliberate status code; resolves store/product ids on the way in (httpOnly `sn_vid` visitor-id cookie for unique-visitor counting, slug→id so history keys on the immutable id) and writes them with the funnel events in ONE `recordPageViewTap()` after next() — page_view / view_item / begin_checkout / seller_register_view, gated to HTML responses, /admin excluded. The HTML gate now covers the store and product counters too: they used to be written before next(), so a 302 to an unavailable store still counted as a view of it
src/lib/cdn.ts                  ← cdnSrc/cdnSrcSet/cdnCropSrcSet/cdnThumb/cdnFill/cdnCircle (r_max + f_png, NOT f_auto — f_auto may pick JPEG, which has no alpha, so the corners the radius cut come back black) + LIGHTBOX_WIDTHS/BANNER_WIDTHS/BANNER_RATIO — THE one place a raw image URL becomes an optimized one. **cdnSrc asks for a CEILING (`c_limit`), never a target:** a bare `w_` is `c_scale`, which invents pixels above the source, so every rung past the seller's own resolution was softer AND heavier than the photo (measured on the demo cloud, 864px source at w_2048: 202KB invented vs 97KB real). cdnFill/cdnCircle keep plain `c_fill` — an ad platform and a favicon slot demand an exact size — and cdnThumb keeps it too, because `c_lfill` was measured to ABANDON the crop ratio once the box exceeds the source. **cdnCropSrcSet is for any `object-fit: cover` box**: a cover frame throws away every pixel outside its ratio AFTER paying to download it, so crop at the CDN (measured on the 3/1 banner against a square source: 84.5KB→32.2KB at w_1600). The width lists live here because the markup, the `<head>` preload and image-derive.ts must all request the identical URL (Cloudinary upload transform + fetch delivery for any other host). Also cdnIsSampleable(url) — whether the delivery URL is CORS-readable, i.e. safe to tag `crossorigin` and read pixels back from; it lives here because that is a Cloudinary fact. Re-exported from store.config.ts; guarded by tests/image-optimization.test.ts
src/lib/cloudinary.ts           ← thumbUrl → re-export of cdn.ts's cdnThumb (historical import path)
src/lib/store-image.ts          ← STORE_IMAGE_FORMATS (logo/square/portrait/landscape) + resolveStoreImage()/storeImageUrl()/storeMarkPath(), and separately STORE_ICON_FORMATS/ROUND_ICON_FORMATS/storeIconUrl() for the browser's own icon slots — a SECOND map on purpose, so a 64px browser icon can never be offered to an ad platform — which picture a store shows per slot, seller upload (via cdnFill, EXACT size or not used at all) → generated mark fallback. `storeAdCreative()` was DELETED 2026-08-09: it returned all four as "the asset group for a scope 'store' boost", had no caller, and named a thing that does not exist — no boost carries a store-level image (the ⚠️ note on STORE_IMAGE_FORMATS holds the three proofs). OG_IMAGE_FORMAT feeds store+product OG tags. Also pairedImageSource() — which uncropped original belongs with the image a settings save settled on; it is not an independent field, so it follows the merge instead of being merged
src/lib/store-mark.ts           ← storeMark(seed,name) — deterministic generated store identity (hue from MARK_HUES + 5×5 grid) so a store with no logo still has a stable one
src/lib/dominant-color.ts       ← dominantGlowColor(rgbaPixels) — the one colour a picture is "about", for the store-card avatar halo. Pure/DOM-free. NOT an average (averaging a mark on a white plate returns near-white, i.e. an invisible halo): TWO steps, neither an average: coloured pixels vote in hue buckets (white/black/grey and near-transparent ones abstain, under 2% coloured returns null), then only the most saturated QUARTER of the winning hue's own pixels decides the shade — a downscaled sample blends every edge pixel of a mark with the white behind it, so the mean of the winning hue is a dusty version of it, fine behind an avatar at 13% and plainly wrong as a 1px border. Clamped into the band a glow stays visible in
src/lib/store-mark-raster.ts    ← renderStoreMarkPixels/renderStoreMarkPng — the same mark as a real PNG, for OG/social crawlers that can't run CSS. renderStoreMarkIconPng is the round tab-icon variant: the mask writes ONLY alpha, so a tab and an ad creative can never disagree about a store's colour, and its edge reuses the blocks' subsample grid so a 16px circle doesn't staircase
src/lib/png.ts                  ← encodePng()/encodePngRgba() — minimal dependency-free PNG encoder used only by store-mark-raster. Alpha is a second entry point rather than a flag: everything leaving here for an ad network or a scraper is opaque by contract, so a caller has to say "rgba" to get it
src/lib/chip-scroller.ts        ← one-line scrolling chip row + floating edge arrows + the row's `.edge-fade` (`data-fade`); one measurement drives both, so fade and arrows can't disagree; store page first, reused by /stores
src/lib/sticky-bar.ts           ← initOverflowShadow: a modal footer's upward shadow ONLY while content is really scrolled behind it, AND it turns the scroller off entirely below a 4px noise floor (sub-pixel overflow out of flex/aspect-ratio rounding is not content, and it varies with zoom/DPR — a 2-line description used to offer a scroll that moved 3 empty pixels). Listeners not a sentinel: a modal's body is replaced by innerHTML, which would discard one. Treatment lives in `.overflow-shadow`, utilities/utils.css; both product modals use it. initStickyBar: toggles .is-stuck when a sticky bar meets the fixed header (fades the store page's glass backdrop in, and tells the header to drop its own shadow — a bar pinned flush beneath it means the header is covering nothing). Also initHeaderScrolled: same IO trick with an absolutely-positioned sentinel at the true document top, marking the header .is-stuck the moment the page scrolls at all
src/lib/admin-threads-query.ts  ← admin Messages-tab toolbar filter/sort over AdminThread[] (twin of seller-messages-query.ts)
src/lib/toolbar-portal.ts       ← createFloatingPortal() — shared floating dropdown (flip-above/reposition-on-scroll, outside-click) for sort/filter toolbars + order-status + perf range picker; + toolbarMenuTitle()/filterClearButtonHtml() (menu heading, and the clear-filter row disabled when nothing to clear). ONE instance serves every trigger in a toolbar, and moving between two never passes through close() — so `open()` hands aria-expanded over itself, or the old pill stays lit (`tests/toolbar-portal.test.ts`)
src/lib/table-column-lock.ts    ← lock/unlockTableColumns() — pins a table's own width + column widths in px so filtering can't resize the columns under an open dropdown; releases on viewport resize
src/lib/home-feed.ts            ← buildHomeFeed() — homepage shelf-selection strategy (liked/buyAgain/new/category/discovery/spotlight), seeded shuffle
src/lib/launch-mode.ts          ← launch-mode thresholds + planLaunchShelf() — what the launch shelf holds: real stores, then ONE showcase store, then placeholders, never all-stores (pure)
src/lib/placeholder-art.ts      ← 12 soft product SVG paths + the ELEVEN `--color-invite-*` hues (a separate list from MARK_HUES — see the invite-card bullet), each with its own wash cap and family, plus the one light direction every tile is lit from (pure)
src/lib/demo-stores.ts          ← isDemoStore/realStores/countRealStores/filterShopperStores + DEMO_HIDE_AT_REAL_STORES — the showcase-store rules (pure); every exclusion (sitemap/feed/IndexNow/checkout/launch threshold) keys off this
src/lib/img-skeleton.ts         ← clearSkeletonOnLoad()/initImageSkeletons() — THE shimmer wiring for every surface (homepage, /stores, /search, store page cards + banner, product page hero + thumbs; the seller dashboard's six image boxes via `.dash-img-skel` since 2026-08-07; the store page's two had hand-rolled copies until 2026-08-03). STOPS the animation only; the image's visibility is CSS-only (see Hard rules → Images). **It also decides when the shimmer may START:** the class is stripped on init and returned when the tile is within `SHIMMER_START_MARGIN` (1600px) of the viewport, i.e. about when the lazy fetch begins — it WAS 250px, and that guess made the whole feature silently inert (measured 2026-08-04: zero shimmers on a full throttled scroll, because every tile was already complete by then). Without that, a tile the browser has not requested — 78 of them on the homepage, off-screen inside horizontal shelves — animates a grey band forever, and since the shimmer sits UNDER the picture that band shows THROUGH a background-removed product and reads as a skeleton painted on it
src/lib/scroll-reveal.ts        ← playReveal()/armBelowFoldReveal() — shared card entrance (fade+rise), homepage + /stores
src/lib/product-search-text.ts  ← productSearchSource() — the ONE definition of a product's search haystack (name, tags, searchable variant values, in that order). Ported into SQL by migration 0027 and pinned character-for-character by tests/product-search-normalize.test.ts. Deliberately WIDER than tag-suggest.ts#isMeaningfulValue, whose job is stored tags; both files say why
src/lib/site-search.ts          ← searchSite() — platform-wide store+product search; configurable storeLimit/productLimit/imageWidth (header dropdown vs. /search page). The PRODUCT half is an indexed query (store-products.ts#searchVisibleProducts) since §3 — it used to read the whole catalogue on every keystroke; the store half stays in memory over the shopper roster. Query capped at 200 chars: /api/search is unauthenticated
src/workers/bg-removal.ts       ← BG removal Web Worker (@imgly, mask-on-small + apply-to-full-res)
src/scripts/dashboard/          ← modules: bulk-selection (products-table selection state: counted/acted-on = ticked AND rendered, hidden ids remembered, "select all" is a mode), promotions (מבצעים tab + the bulk discount dialog), discount-field (delegated behaviour for the discount block), bg-worker (background removal runs IN THE BROWSER — @imgly model in a Worker, no provider, no quota, no cost. Every path assumed the worker answers, so a worker the browser KILLED — an out-of-memory model load on a weak phone — left the busy button spinning forever with no error: `error`/`messageerror` listeners cover the crash shapes, and a 90s INACTIVITY timer covers silence. Inactivity, never duration: the first run downloads a model and can legitimately take minutes on cellular, but progress ticks arrive throughout), cleanup-modal, cloudinary (the ONE upload path, seller + admin. Reads the provider's moderation verdict via lib/image-moderation.ts, and marks the four errors a seller must ACT on — empty/format/too-large/refused — with `name = UPLOAD_REFUSED`, because the error copy in products.ts wrapped all of them in "try again", which is wrong advice for every one: the same file fails the same way. A dropped connection stays a retry), status, crop-modal (pan/zoom crop; `round` draws the circle the avatar is actually rendered in — PREVIEW only, the blob stays the full square because that is what StoreAvatar and the alpha-less ad feeds consume), gallery, products (CRUD + hide/show toggle + live stock-badge), ui, store-image (the store banner + avatar widget: crop on upload, "התאם תמונה" re-frames what is already saved from the uncropped original, and it surfaces the upload provider's own failure sentence instead of dropping it), csv-import, csv-preview, feed-sync (external-inventory panel: column mapping UI + file/URL import + tokenized outbound-feed share controls), category-tree (Settings tree editor), category-tree-cache (shared cache, avoids products.ts↔category-picker.ts circular import), panel-intent ("open that tab with this already applied" — the overview's tiles record one and the target panel's init collects it, so neither reaches into the other), category-picker (nested dropdown-in-dropdown), performance (range-picker+chart fetch, responsive-width repaint, revenue→donut modal, per-product picker+drilldown), advertising (campaign CRUD; reads data-endpoint → also drives admin per-store ad page), select-dropdown (upgrades a native <select> to the floating-portal dropdown; opts: triggerClassName, triggerLabel fixed-label, menuAutoWidth content-sized popup, menuHeading a role=presentation title row — a compact trigger shows only its value, so a phone-width popup was bare numbers, optionMeta per-option trailing text e.g. price; it also copies the select's aria-label onto the trigger it generates, which is the element assistive tech actually reaches), list-pager (THE pager for every AJAX-paged list — seller products/orders/messages and the buyer's orders. renderListPagers/initListPager/markListBusy/createFetchGate; no language literals, every string arrives from the caller, which is what lets orders.ts share it under tests/orders-i18n.test.ts. A tab mounts it TWICE via data-list-pager and one write fills both; the middle is one page-select, never a strip of numbers; the click awaits apply() before it scrolls, and createFetchGate stops a slow answer landing after a newer one), scroll-utils (animateScrollTo — RTL-safe JS-computed smooth scroll, shared by products.ts + advertising.ts; scrollProductsPanelIntoView — lands a products-tab panel below the 3 sticky layers, only when its top is hidden; scrollRowBackIntoView — returns a collapsed edit row to the viewport after save/cancel, no-op when it is already fully visible), unsaved-guard (one place that knows what is unsaved, answering three questions: beforeunload = "you are about to lose this"; the floating notice = "did I save that", raised only for a section that is OFF SCREEN; discardChanges = "how do I get back". A form opts in with data-unsaved-guard, dirty = current values differ from the per-field baseline. Discard writes that baseline back rather than calling form.reset(), which restores NOTHING on a `type="hidden"` input — its `.value` and `defaultValue` are the same attribute, and the cropper + both category pickers live in hidden fields. announceValueChange() is the ONE way to announce a programmatic field write: assigning `.value` fires no event at all, so every widget doing it is invisible to the notice unless it says so. Discard emits `dash:fieldsrewritten` (repaint — the draft restore emits it too) AND `dash:discarded` (thrown away on purpose — this one deletes the stored draft, so a restore must not fire it)), tab-sync (multi-tab: initDashTabSync observes fetch ONCE and broadcasts every successful dashboard mutation; a receiving tab live-refreshes the ACTIVE panel through the refresher that panel registered with registerPanelRefresh — products/orders/messages do — and only while nothing is open/typed, else raises StaleDataBar; also markDashboardStale + conflictMessage), tab-alert-edges (a `.dash-tabs` strip that overflows hides its own markers, so the edge fade grows a dot in the colour of whichever marker is off-screen that way; every marker declares `data-tab-alert="danger|warning"` itself and a MutationObserver drives it, so no writer has to call back)
src/components/HomeShelf.astro  ← homepage shelf section (title + StoreCard row); `hideHeading` for single-shelf tabs, `invites` appends placeholder cards, `categoryLabel` swaps the heading dot for that category's icon
src/components/HomeTabEmpty.astro ← homepage per-tab empty-state (icon+message+CTA)
src/components/StoreCard.astro  ← compact store card — homepage shelves + /stores directory
src/components/StorePlaceholderCard.astro ← open-slot card in the same `.store-card` geometry (launch mode)
src/components/StoreSaleBanner.astro ← store-wide sale strip; renders on the store page AND every product page of that store, nothing at all unless the sale is live. `.card`'s recipe + the green figure, and deliberately nothing else — its CSS comment lists the nine decorative treatments tried and rejected (2026-07-30), read it before restyling. Takes a `class` prop for per-page spacing (a wrapper div would leave a gap when no sale is live)
src/components/OwnerStoreBar.astro ← wayfinding strip for a seller standing in his own store (store + product pages); the page under it is the live storefront, only buying is refused
src/components/CategoryIcon.astro ← the inline-SVG artwork behind category-icons.ts; `label` (resolved) or `name` (explicit, e.g. the "all" chip) + `size`
src/components/StoreCategoryPicker.astro ← store-category picker (chips + search + guarded "add new"); submits the same comma `categories` field the old text input did
src/components/StoreDemoBadge.astro ← "חנות לדוגמה" label; `quiet` variant for rows that are already busy (spotlight header)
src/components/StoreDemoBanner.astro ← showcase store's banner: the PHOTOGRAPH, and nothing else. THREE overlays were built and deleted — an SVG clipped to "הר" by `preserveAspectRatio=slice`, an HTML one that put Hebrew on the LEFT (in an RTL column flex `align-items:flex-end` IS the left edge), and finally a correct one that was still wrong because the generated banner now carries the name itself. The scrim went with it: it existed only to make white type legible, and darkening a third of a photograph for type that no longer exists is just a worse photograph
src/components/StoreDemoMark.astro   ← the drawn FALLBACK mark. The showcase stores now carry generated logos (`identity.mjs#logoPrompt`) and this is what any store without one still gets. One flat form each in the store's own accent. Generated emblems came back as photographs of objects (cropped to a circle, soft at 56px) and the built-in monogram gave "ס on a green circle" in an unrelated hue. Holds its own palette keyed by slug — it was a prop and the header forgot it, so one shop had two colours (tests/showcase-mark-palette.test.ts)
src/components/StoreDemoBar.astro    ← the "חנות לדוגמה" disclosure as a strip above the banner, same shape as OwnerStoreBar, scrolling away on the first gesture. It was a badge + link + tinted card inside the store's identity block and pushed the shop's own name down the page
src/pages/robots.txt.ts        ← SSR (NOT a public/ file — it is host-dependent): a seller's verified domain gets ITS OWN sitemap line and no platform one; /api/feed/ invited on the platform host only; the AI-crawler list lives here and bot-detect.test.ts imports it
src/pages/sitemap-content.xml.ts ← the content sitemap for pages @astrojs/sitemap cannot see (prerender=false); blocked-gated, listed in robots.txt. **Serves, does not build (2026-08-09)**, and on the platform host it is now a `<sitemapindex>` over numbered shards — a sitemap FILE is capped at 50,000 URLs and rejected whole above it. The custom-domain branch IS still built per request and is still a plain urlset: one store's shelf, keyed by a host the platform build knows nothing about
src/pages/sitemap-content-[shard].xml.ts ← one shard of that index (`/sitemap-content-1.xml`). Serves a stored artifact and builds nothing; an out-of-range shard is 404
src/pages/llms.txt.ts          ← dynamic /llms.txt (llmstxt.org) — AI-engine-readable site map, derived from store.config so the real domain propagates on its own
src/pages/[key].txt.ts         ← serves the IndexNow verification file at /{seo.indexNowKey}.txt; 404s until a key is configured
src/pages/index.astro           ← SSR homepage
src/pages/404.astro             ← not-found page
src/pages/terms.astro           ← תנאי שימוש. Hebrew in both UI languages (stated as binding); clauses are DATA, one array. States no returns window on purpose — that decision is open, and a guess here is a public promise. Reserved slug + linked from every footer: a 404 here is the Merchant Center "misrepresentation" class, which suspends the ACCOUNT
src/pages/contact.astro         ← יצירת קשר — operator identity, the email channel, and the report form. Captures the page you came from via `?from=` or Referer (hence `Vary: Referer`). Same reason it exists as a page, same reserved-slug rule
src/pages/stores.astro          ← SSR /stores directory — search/category-filter/pagination
src/pages/search.astro          ← SSR /search results — stores (StoreCard) + products, back button (history.back with same-origin-referrer check)
src/pages/[storeSlug]/index.astro    ← SSR store page (301s an old slug via previousSlugs; 301s to the custom domain when active)
src/pages/[storeSlug]/[productSlug].astro ← SSR product page (same previous-slug + custom-domain 301s as the store page)
src/pages/seller/               ← register, login, dashboard (?panel=X URL sync)
src/pages/buyer/dashboard.astro ← buyer area (?tab=X URL sync)
src/pages/checkout.astro + checkout/success.astro ← SSR checkout + confirmation
src/pages/admin/index.astro     ← admin dashboard shell, 8 tabs (incl. advertising), per-tab pagination/filter query-param parsing; advertising tab parses ?adpreset/adfrom/adto (range) + builds overview vs previous period
src/pages/admin/login.astro + logout.ts ← admin auth (ADMIN_SECRET env — 'admin' in dev only; a hard failure in production, see admin-auth.ts)
src/pages/admin/store/[slug]/performance.astro ← admin-guarded per-store performance (reuses seller perf markup+libs; data-endpoint=/api/admin/performance)
src/pages/admin/store/[slug]/advertising.astro ← admin-guarded per-store advertising — the seller tab's twin: same BoostFormFields + CampaignHistory + advertising.ts, its own category-tree-data payload for the scope picker, data-endpoint=/api/admin/ad-campaigns
src/components/admin/           ← AdminPanelShell (the container for ALL eleven tabs — rendered whatever happens, since DashTabsBoot toggles `hidden` on it, swapPanel replaces its innerHTML and wirePanelLinks binds one delegated listener to it;
