only the CONTENTS are conditional, and `data-lazy` is its contract with scripts/admin/lazy-panels.ts) + AdminOverviewPanel/SellersPanel/StoresPanel/OrdersPanel/AttentionPanel/MessagesPanel/AlertsPanel (display-only, one per tab) + AdminModerationStatus (one component, `placement="overview"|"alerts"`; the alerts one renders NOTHING while healthy) + AdminReportsPanel (the visitor-report queue, above the Alerts tab's error table) + AdminPager (shared arrow-pager, all 6 list tabs) + AdminNewBadge/AdminNewOnlyToggle (the "new since you last left this tab" row chip + filter chip, shared by the 4 tracked tabs) + AdminAdvertisingPanel (platform advertising tab: range picker, exposure bar chart/PPC insights, brand-campaign manager, promoted-stores control) + AdminPerformancePanel (app-wide performance tab: KPIs+charts, per-store breakdown table, GMV/commission split; reuses the seller #perf-* DOM + shared performance.ts client) + AdminMoneyLogPanel (יומן כספי tab: AdminReconciliationCard (clean = ONE muted line; only a problem gets the full red card — a banner that always says "fine" is furniture, and it trains the eye to skip where the alarm will appear), AdminMoneyLogToolbar (search + a sectioned event-type MENU + date window + clear; thirteen always-visible chips had stopped saying what the tab was for), then the append-only journal grouped by business day — no standing explanation above it: a multi-store purchase is one charge + one order per store, and each row says which slice it is — each row carries its own date, a copyable `?mev=` permalink, and order/checkout refs that link to their own timeline; AdminPager, 15/page) + AdminDataPanel (first-party analytics tab: buyer funnel, abandonment/conversion rate cards, top added/abandoned products, seller onboarding funnel; range = trigger+portal dropdown → swapPanel)
```
src/scripts/admin/               ← orders-filter, admin-messages, admin-msg-seller-dropdown, sellers (block toggle + message modal + search), stores (block/unblock + search + promote-cycle), alerts, advertising-platform (range picker via swapPanel, baseline-budget form, brand-campaign CRUD + Cloudinary upload + site-design selects, promoted-store retune/cancel, exposure-chart repaint+tooltip w/ observer cleanup), platform-performance (breakdown table search/sort/paging via its own AJAX + both money cards, synced on `perf:loaded`, params pinned via picker.dataset.extraParams), data-panel (data tab: range dropdown → swapPanel, delegated on the surviving container, singleton portal) — client wiring; search/sort/filter navigate server-side (debounced), not DOM-filter
src/scripts/tooltip.ts          ← the floating tooltips: showTooltip/showTooltipAtPoint/showSeriesTooltips/hideTooltip/mountTooltipIn + initInfoTooltips() for [data-tooltip]. Lived under scripts/dashboard/ until icon-tooltips gave it site-wide callers. Sets textContent, NEVER innerHTML — labels carry seller-supplied product names. A POOL, not a singleton: showSeriesTooltips gives a multi-series chart one box PER series, beside its own point and in its own colour (one box explaining both read as a single sentence in a single colour, and the colour was one of the two); topmost goes above, the rest below, so they cannot overlap; every single-tooltip entry point hides the extras or one is left stranded. Re-attaches when detached — `mountTooltipIn` parks these inside a &lt;dialog&gt;, so removing that subtree used to kill every later tooltip on the page, silently
src/scripts/nav-progress.ts     ← initNavProgress() (BaseLayout): the `.progress-sweep` bar under the header while a full navigation is in flight. This is an MPA, so between the click and the next paint nothing on screen says a request exists. Armed on a link click (skips defaultPrevented / modified / hash / _blank / off-site) and on submit; waits 450ms before drawing anything, so a fast navigation and every bfcache back show nothing at all. Cleared on pageshow (a bfcache restore brings the running bar back with it) and on Escape
src/scripts/icon-tooltips.ts    ← initIconTooltips() (BaseLayout): ONE delegated listener showing any icon-only control's own aria-label on hover, 450ms. Skips [data-tooltip]/[title], [data-no-tooltip] (the explicit opt-out, for a control whose CONTENT is the thing — the product page's photo and thumbnails), an open disclosure (aria-expanded=true — its panel is often INSIDE the trigger), and a control whose label is already on screen. Reads innerText not textContent: the dashboard toolbars hide their label span with CSS, so the buttons that go icon-only are exactly the ones textContent called labelled. Never binds without (hover:hover)+(pointer:fine). Consequence to remember: an aria-label is now VISIBLE TEXT — a hardcoded English one is a bug (tests/icon-tooltips.test.ts scans for it)
src/scripts/share-menu.ts       ← initShareMenus() — ShareMenu.astro's behaviour, and everything in it is an ENHANCEMENT (the network links work without it): open/close, Escape, Up/Down/Home/End, clipboard with a textarea fallback for a non-secure origin, the OS sheet, and a GA4 `share` event carrying the catalog item_id or omitting it (never empty — lib/tracking.ts's `reportable` rule). Delegated from `document` and keyed by attribute, so a page with two menus needs no second wiring. `focusout` only closes when `relatedTarget` is a real element: browsers that do not focus a clicked anchor report null, and closing there hides the item between mousedown and mouseup so the click never fires. `positionPanel` keeps 12px off the viewport edge (it was 17px OFF-screen at 768) by nudging a physical `left` — never `transform`, which the entrance animation owns and which outranks an inline style for its whole duration
src/scripts/store-glow.ts       ← initStoreGlow() — self-guarded against a second caller on one page; sets `--store-glow` on the nearest `[data-glow-host]` (the store card and a store's own header; the spotlight header was tried and cannot hold a halo — see home.css) from its avatar's own pixels (dominant-color.ts) via a shared 24×24 canvas, at idle, memoised per image URL in sessionStorage (values re-validated as `#rrggbb` on the way back OUT of storage). Costs no extra request: it samples the image the page already loads. Every failure — no CORS, tainted canvas, greyscale logo, no JS — falls through to the CSS default, so the halo is never absent, only less personal. Lives on StoreCard.astro's own `<script>` so it ships only with pages that draw the halo
src/scripts/csrf-client.ts      ← initCsrf() — wraps `window.fetch` ONCE to attach the CSRF token (from BaseLayout's <meta>) to every same-origin non-GET request, instead of remembering it at ~130 call sites. Loaded from BaseLayout's <head>, and the position is load-bearing: bundled scripts are deferred modules run in document order, so this one precedes cart-sync and the error reporter. dashboard/tab-sync.ts wraps fetch too, as an observer, and composes because it initialises later
src/scripts/error-reporter.ts   ← initErrorReporter() (window.onerror/unhandledrejection) + reportClientError() (manual report from a caught path) → /api/log-client-error, capped 5/session
src/pages/api/lang.ts           ← POST language switch (he/en cookie) → 303 back to the referring page, same-origin only (safeRefererPath)
src/pages/api/user-cart.ts      ← GET/POST the logged-in user's server-side cart + wishlist + recentStores (session-guarded, own id only)
src/pages/api/favorite-store.ts ← POST favourite-store toggle (session-guarded); tolerates a previous slug, always stores the CURRENT one
src/pages/api/saved-stores.ts   ← GET the session's saved stores + name/logo/mark for the header's avatar menu (isStoreVisible, capped at 6 + a `total`); client-fetched, never SSR'd into the header
src/pages/api/messages.ts       ← GET/POST messages
src/pages/api/notifications.ts  ← GET/POST notifications
src/pages/api/admin-messages.ts ← seller-facing admin-thread GET/POST (session-guarded, own sellerId only); a seller's reply here shares /api/messages' flood buckets, or the system thread is an unlimited write path beside a limited one
src/pages/api/admin/messages.ts ← admin-facing GET/POST (admin-guarded); api/admin/errors.ts ← GET/clear error log
src/pages/api/admin/performance.ts ← admin-guarded per-store PerformanceSummary (twin of api/seller/performance, any store by slug; read-only); `?productId=` → per-product drill-down (store-scoped validation)
src/pages/api/admin/platform-performance.ts ← admin-guarded app-wide PerformanceSummary + income split (platform-revenue) + ONE page of breakdown rows (?storeQ/storeSort/storeDir/storePage); drives the admin ביצועים tab's range picker AND its table search/sort/paging
src/pages/api/admin/moderation.ts ← admin-guarded block/unblock store/product; notifies + messages the seller (appeal channel)
src/pages/api/admin/store-products.ts ← admin-guarded per-store product list (server-searched + paginated 8/page, Cloudinary thumbs) — Stores-tab expandable block list source
src/pages/api/admin/ad-campaigns.ts ← admin-guarded twin of api/seller/ad-campaigns (any store by slug); full boost-campaign CRUD
src/pages/api/admin/promote.ts  ← admin-guarded: set a store's promoWeight (silent "shop-window" curation; NO seller notification, opposite of moderation.ts)
src/pages/api/admin/platform-ads.ts ← admin-guarded: update baseline campaign lifetime budget + active/paused (platform-ads.ts)
src/pages/api/admin/brand-campaigns.ts ← admin-guarded platform-brand campaign CRUD (GET+stats/POST/PATCH/DELETE); parseCreateInput sanitizes destination scheme + image https
src/pages/api/log-client-error.ts ← unauthenticated client-error intake, body-size + field-length capped
src/pages/api/report.ts         ← unauthenticated visitor fault/content report. Rate-limited per address (reportRules) — and it is the one limiter that counts ACCEPTED calls, since a flood of valid reports is the abuse. An unusable email is dropped, never a reason to refuse the report
src/pages/api/admin/reports.ts  ← admin triage: mark one handled / put it back. No clear action — a person wrote every row
src/pages/api/health.ts         ← GET → 200 serving / 503 not, for an EXTERNAL uptime monitor. Unauthenticated (a monitor cannot log in) with a 5s answer cache so it is not a way to drain the pool. HEALTH_PATH is short-circuited in middleware BELOW the CSRF gate — everything else there touches the DB, so without it the endpoint 500s in exactly the outage it exists for
src/pages/api/analytics/event.ts ← unauthenticated funnel intake (POST add_to_cart only, strict whitelist, vid from httpOnly cookie not body, capped)
src/pages/api/seller/alerts.ts  ← GET {hasAlert} for header avatar dot
src/pages/api/seller/orders.ts  ← GET: no `?page` = legacy full unpaginated list (poll's new-order detection needs the whole set); `?page` = paginated+searched+sorted JSON. PATCH: order edit (status/tracking/buyer details/item soft-delete/discount)
src/pages/api/seller/products.ts ← GET paginated+searched+sorted+filtered JSON (Products tab AJAX) + store-wide stockAlerts count for the live tab badge
src/pages/api/product.ts        ← seller product CRUD actions incl. set-product-visibility (hidden toggle); mutating actions return fresh stockAlerts count
src/pages/api/seller/messages.ts ← GET paginated+searched+sorted+filtered JSON (Messages tab AJAX, buyer threads only)
src/pages/api/seller/performance.ts ← GET {storeSlug,from,to,granularity?,products?} → PerformanceSummary JSON, range capped 731 days; products=all uncaps topProducts; `?productId=` → single-product ProductPerformanceSummary (validates product belongs to the store)
src/pages/api/seller/ad-campaigns.ts ← GET live list + `archived` history, both with stats (shared resolveAdRange: dated preset → windowed, lifetime/no-preset → per-campaign lifetime via withCampaignStats), POST create (buildCampaignInput; audience dropped for store scope), PATCH budget/status (resume refused 409 CAMPAIGN_UNAVAILABLE/OUT_OF_STOCK/ENDED), DELETE = archive, never erase. Admin twin (api/admin/ad-campaigns.ts) shares all of it
src/pages/api/feed/products.xml.ts ← public Google Merchant / Meta Catalog feed (RSS 2.0), all visible products, per-product derived attrs + variant item_group_id rows; blocked-gated. **Serves the pre-built artifact and assembles nothing (2026-08-09)** — feed-document.ts builds it, catalog-artifacts.ts stores and streams it
src/pages/api/search.ts         ← GET site-wide search (stores+products) for the header dropdown, wraps site-search.ts
src/pages/api/buyer/orders.ts   ← GET buyer Orders tab, paginated+searched+active/history-split JSON of PURCHASES (buyer-purchases.ts); the flat `items` beside it is one deploy's backward compatibility, drop it later
src/pages/api/user/update-profile.ts ← POST account details (name/email/password) for the logged-in seller/buyer session
src/pages/api/store-product/featured.ts ← the seller's store-card pick. Its OWN route, not a field on /api/product: the per-store cap is enforced inside setProductFeatured's single UPDATE, and a second way to write the column is a way past that statement. Answers the resulting COUNT so the dashboard needs no tally of its own
src/pages/api/                  ← product, store, wishlist, lang, user-cart, checkout, cart/prices.ts (re-prices cart lines the way /api/checkout will — cart-prices.ts's server side; also answers each line's stock, per variant combo, withheld when the request named none); store-product.ts (quick-view/modal data source — also recordProductView() for the per-product views metric); store-search-index.ts (ONE store's visible products, reduced to the five fields the header-search dropdown paints — replaced the `#header-search-products` island that serialised the whole catalog into every store and product page, 24.1KB on the biggest showcase store. Header.astro fetches it once on idle after paint and on first focus, so the matching stays local and instant and only the bytes left the critical path)
src/pages/api/store-product/bulk.ts ← GET export CSV / POST dry-run preview + commit import (thin — delegates to runProductImport)
src/pages/api/store-product/feed-sync.ts ← POST "sync now": authenticates the seller, proves they own the store, reads the body — then hands off to store-feed-sync.ts, which the scheduler's feed-sync job also calls (preview + commit)
src/pages/api/store-image/[slug]/[file].ts ← serves /api/store-image/<slug>/<format>.png — generated store mark as a real PNG (whitelisted sizes only, real slug only, immutable cache); the tab-icon slot comes out round with transparent corners, every other format is the opaque tile
src/pages/api/store-feed/[token].ts ← GET outbound per-store inventory feed (CSV default / ?format=json), token IS the credential; noindex+no-store, 404 on bad/blocked token
src/pages/api/store-category.ts ← POST create/rename/delete/move-category (authorizes via getStoresBySellerId, returns fresh tree)
src/pages/api/auth/google.ts    ← OAuth initiation
src/pages/api/auth/google/callback.ts ← OAuth callback
src/styles/main.css             ← single CSS entry point
src/styles/base/tokens.css      ← CSS variables: colors/font/shadows/max-width in `:root`; radius scale + animations in `@theme` (Tailwind namespace — see Hard rules)
src/styles/base/reset.css
src/styles/layout/container.css
src/styles/components/          ← buttons, cards, forms, header, footer, cart-drawer, store-card, etc.
src/styles/pages/               ← home, auth, dashboard, store, product, checkout, stores, search
src/styles/utilities/utils.css  ← .muted, .badge, skeleton-shimmer, msg-thread, notif-dot, dot-pulse loader, .edge-fade (scroll-row edge mask, `data-fade`), .store-glow-wrap (the store-colour avatar halo; the consumer owns its own `:hover` trigger, and the colour is written onto the nearest `[data-glow-host]` by scripts/store-glow.ts)
src/env.d.ts                    ← global Window types (dataLayer, fbq, __sessionUserId, etc.)
.claudeignore                   ← skip node_modules, dist, _backup, pics, fonts
scripts/check-required-env.mjs   ← npm `prestart` gate: refuses to boot without AUTH_SECRET/ADMIN_SECRET or while they are still the public dev defaults, naming the one that's missing. `npm start` = serve the BUILD (it used to alias `astro dev` — the command a host runs by default, where PROD is false and every missing secret silently takes its dev fallback). This is what finally makes GO_LIVE §7's "fails at boot" true; until now the guards threw on the first request touching a session, which the visitor sees as a redirect and an operator sees as nothing
astro.config.mjs                ← node adapter, Tailwind vite plugin, passthrough image service; vite.server.allowedHosts ['.test'] (custom-domain testing) + vite.server.watch.ignored ['**/data/**'] — the app WRITES pageviews into data/ while serving, so watching it made every page view trigger a reload that served the page again (dev-only; the build watches nothing)
vitest.config.ts                ← node env default; per-file `// @vitest-environment jsdom` where DOM/localStorage is touched
tests/runtime-env.test.ts       ← serverEnv precedence/blank-is-unset, requiredSecret, + a scan of src/ banning direct import.meta.env.<SERVER_VAR> reads

tests/crop-pan-range.test.ts     ← panRange: a CONTAINED image may travel to the frame edge (the flush-right case that `Math.max(0, …)` pinned to dead centre), and a covering one moves exactly as it always did
tests/store-header-logo.test.ts ← the no-stretch guarantee as declarations (fixed 32px height, the 11rem cap, `contain` not `cover`, `min-width:0`) plus storeHeaderLogo's two-column rule — a rendered-pixel test would need a browser and would still only try the ratios it thought of
tests/coupons.test.ts           ← the pure half: code normalization (Hebrew dropped — deliberately NOT the slug rule), percent/₪ arithmetic in integer agorot, the clamp to [0, subtotal] that keeps a ₪50 voucher on a ₪30 cart out of reconcile's corrupt-row report, inclusive endsAt, and every parse refusal
tests/store-coupons-db.test.ts  ← against a real Postgres: 50 concurrent claims of a max_uses=10 code let exactly 10 through, release floors at zero, the unique index decides a duplicate, a foreign store id reaches nothing, and storesWithLiveCoupons agrees with isCouponLive
tests/product-stock-cas.test.ts ← inline stock edits are compare-and-set: refused (409 + the real number) when a sale moved stock since the cell rendered, per-combo isolated, name/price ungated

An unrecognised combo falling through to the shared pool is an oversell, not a fallback
tests/checkout.test.ts          ← /api/checkout price/shipping re-validation (incl. delivery-method: platform courier rate default, free self-pickup, spoofed/unavailable method → courier fallback), signed-in cart cleanup, atomic stock decrement/rollback, the 409 out-of-stock payload (which line, live count, variant combo), low/out-of-stock notification crossing+mutual-exclusivity, blocked+hidden product rejection

Found 2 live copies the moment it was written

tests/analytics-db.test.ts      ← the funnel against real Postgres: a session counted ONCE across the range, volume summed, one call naming a product twice adding two, volume with no session ids still reported, an impossible date answering empty instead of 500, and the abandonment ranking ordered by the GAP before the LIMIT
tests/seller-funnel.test.ts     ← buildSellerFunnel (per-stage counts, monotonic funnel, multi-store dedup, empty-platform zero-fill)
tests/seller-products-query.test.ts ← stockBucket boundary (LOW_STOCK_THRESHOLD) + pstock parse + stock-filter OR semantics
tests/seller-messages-query.test.ts ← Messages-tab filter/sort/search + pinned admin thread
tests/seller-orders-query.test.ts ← filterAndSortSellerOrders urgency-sort grouping (owe-action oldest-first) + search/status-filter, one-store subtotal scope
tests/pricing.test.ts           ← tier table shape + resolveTier/commissionPercentForTier/monthlyFeeForTier; asserts upgrade break-evens RISE across tiers (no dominated tier)
tests/record-rev.test.ts        ← which changes a revision notices vs ignores (both directions), + mergeByFieldRev: two tabs on different fields BOTH win, only a same-field clash conflicts, force settles just that field, no-baseline = whole-form write
tests/seller-alerts.test.ts     ← the per-store dot's SEVERITY against a real DB: danger outranks the warning under it and falls back to it when the order is handled, the header's map stays danger-only, a hidden product never nags, a self-healing (sold-out) campaign pause is not a dot. Plus a source scan pinning that every `data-tab-alert` in the dashboard is either in `PER_STORE_TABS` or the one deliberate account-wide exclusion
tests/seller-onboarding.test.ts ← buildOnboardingSteps/onboardingProgress + the required-steps discoverability gate
tests/store-readiness.test.ts   ← buildStoreReadiness/isStoreReady — the discovery gate (no visible product → out of sitemap/search, never 404)
tests/dash-tabs-boot.test.ts    ← every page with a tab strip renders <DashTabsBoot />, activation is not re-implemented in ui.ts, the boot stays inline (a fetch would defeat it), a tab is revealed by scrolling the STRIP not the page, and the waiting pulse is cleared BEFORE the replayed click
tests/skeleton-classes.test.ts  ← no placeholder class that a stylesheet never defines, the @keyframes name is never used as one, and .skel-bar really carries the animation — a loader that renders nothing is invisible on the page and reads as "no data"
tests/global-chrome-forms.test.ts ← every method=POST form in Header/Footer/BaseLayout carries data-native-submit — the dashboard's guard judges them too, and blocking one reads to the seller as "saving is unavailable"
tests/orders-i18n.test.ts       ← the orders client rebuild carries no language literals and asks only for keys BOTH dictionaries define; the age chip takes its language from the page, never a constant
tests/buyer-dashboard-i18n.test.ts ← the same class on the buyer's page: every `_i18nEl.dataset.X` the client renderer reads is published by the #buyer-i18n element, every published key exists in both dictionaries, and both status-label maps cover every shippingStatus the Order type has. The fallback is an English literal, so a missing key is silent and only on the AJAX path
tests/buyer-purchases.test.ts   ← one card per PURCHASE over rows checkout split per store: grouping, the total as the sum of its slices, earliest date, least-advanced headline, a cancelled slice inside a live order, the active/history split off the status table, search across every slice, and two purchases refusing to merge on a colliding 8-char checkoutRef
tests/bidi-truncation.test.ts   ← no element both truncates to one line and carries `dir="auto"` — dir sets ALIGNMENT as well as direction, so a Latin name on a Hebrew page left-aligned itself away from the logo it names and ellipsised the wrong end. Derives the truncating class names from the CSS; `<bdi>` is the fix
tests/performance-range-failure.test.ts ← a range switch that fails: the panel stops shimmering and says so even when the failure beats the 180ms placeholder timer, and the picker reverts to the range the figures on screen are from
tests/order-card-layout.test.ts ← the collapsed order card's header is the same layout in all three renderers (seller SSR, client rebuild, admin) and keyed off the CARD's width, not the viewport: the grid cells, the two display:contents wrappers, no `sm:` creeping back, and that the age chip yields while the status badge and the chip's own icon never do
tests/seller-orders-cancel.test.ts ← PATCH /api/seller/orders cancellation (restock + guards)
tests/seller-orders-partial.test.ts ← PATCH /api/seller/orders partial edit: an unnamed field (and the money on it) is untouched — an omitted discount survives an item delete and is re-applied to the new base, an explicit null still clears it
tests/order-emails.test.ts      ← buildBuyerOrderConfirmation/buildSellerOrderNotification content
tests/order-status-email.test.ts ← buildOrderStatusEmail per status (incl. guest orders)
tests/order-notify.test.ts      ← buildOrderStatusNotification — the in-app half of the same pipeline
tests/variant-csv.test.ts       ← mergeVariantGroups grouping/updates/validation
tests/store-image.test.ts       ← store mark identity (stable per seed) + generated mark PNG + cdnFill + the store favicon (round in the tab, square on iOS, never merged into the ad-creative format map, never delivered as alpha-less JPEG) + pairedImageSource (an uncropped original follows the crop the merge chose, and an unchanged picture keeps its stored one whatever the request carries)
tests/brand-lockup.test.ts      ← the lockup is ONE drawing: every generated file carries the module's own paths, no component holds a literal brand path (a three-figure coordinate in a `d=` is the tell — icons never leave a 24-unit box), no generated file contains NaN/Infinity, the mark is still half a REGULAR octagon (45° cut, 1/(2+√2) of its height), the viewBoxes are cropped to the stroked ink, and Chakra Petch is not shipped as a webfont
tests/store-image-widget.test.ts ← the store banner/avatar widget at its SEAMS, not its logic: every id it looks up is rendered (a typo is a silent `null`), the avatar asks for the round crop preview and the banner does not, and each hidden field's `name` is one the API actually reads
tests/indexnow.test.ts          ← ping guards (key/real-domain required) + buildIndexNowPayload + showcase-store guard
tests/demo-stores.test.ts       ← demo identification, real-store threshold counting, shopper discovery filter
tests/image-optimization.test.ts ← cdn.ts delivery rules + a mechanical sweep of src/**: no raw image URL on an <img>, loading+decoding always, no hand-rolled lazy-loading. Also pins `c_limit` on every cdnSrc width and its ABSENCE from cdnFill/cdnThumb
tests/image-container-bg.test.ts ← an image container is --color-surface, never the page or line colour: a transparent product shows its box's fill through the subject. TWO halves, because the first one missed the worst case — the CSS sweep over src/styles/**, and (2026-08-07) a markup sweep over src/**/*.{astro,ts} for a fixed, clipping box (w- + h- + overflow-hidden) filled with --color-bg/--color-border, which is the shape the checkout's cart thumbnail had. Hairline dividers and progress tracks do not match it
tests/unsaved-notice.test.ts    ← jsdom: the notice names the right section, stays silent while the seller is standing IN it, appears on walking away (via `dashtab:show`, since a tab switch fires no input event), and "discard" comes back to the last SAVE — plus the guard that nothing hand-rolls the input event announceValueChange owns
tests/seller-orders-scope.test.ts ← the seller Orders authorization guarantee: another store's order id paired with your own slug is refused for status/buyer-details/item-delete/notes alike, plus a class scan — any api route calling updateOrder must also call orderBelongsToStore
tests/store-ownership.test.ts   ← the seller Catalogue authorization guarantee, mirror of the Orders one: ownedStore/ownedProduct refuse a foreign or blank id (blank never falls back to the first store), keep not-found and not-yours apart because the callers answer 404 vs 403, plus two class scans — nothing under src/pages may reach createProduct/updateProduct/deleteProduct without the module, and nothing may re-scan getStoresBySellerId inline
tests/oauth-verified-email.test.ts ← drives the real Google callback: an unverified (or absent, or non-boolean) `verified_email` links nothing, creates nothing and opens no session — the account-takeover path, since a matching address links straight into an existing seller
tests/order-total-single-source.test.ts ← storeSliceTotal's arithmetic + a scan of src/** for a `.subtotal + …shipping` computed inline again
tests/field-focus-consistency.test.ts ← every text field focuses the same way: border → --color-primary, and no ring at all (a field matches :focus-visible on pointer too)
tests/design-shadow-rule.test.ts ← the elevation rule over src/**: no at-rest --shadow-xs/--shadow-card outside a FLOATING allowlist whose every entry names why that surface floats. Scans stylesheets, .astro <style> blocks, Tailwind `shadow-[…]` utilities and inline styles alike
tests/animation-fill-mode.test.ts ← no `forwards`/`both` on an animation whose last keyframe is just the resting state — that fill sits in the ANIMATION cascade origin and silently outranks :hover forever, which is what stopped the homepage cards lifting (twice). Parses every @keyframes to decide; the load-bearing fills are allowlisted by name
tests/chart-svg.test.ts         ← the two things about a chart that reading the code cannot show: the line is a real curve that NEVER leaves the plot area (the monotone clamp, provoked with spikes and drops to zero, since an overshoot paints revenue below the baseline), the area fill is built from the same `d` as the stroke, and every chart type pins its height inline — a global `svg{height:auto}` in reset.css silently beat the height attribute and made SSR and client render 13.4px apart
tests/lightbox-image.test.ts    ← neighbourIndexes: wrap-around, dedup on a 2-image gallery, next-before-previous, and that it stays ±1 (never the whole gallery) however many images there are
tests/image-derive.test.ts      ← warmImageDerivations: every lightbox width requested, HEAD not GET, dedup + MAX_IMAGES fan-out cap, skips URLs the CDN can't improve, never rejects when the CDN is down
tests/revenue-integrity.test.ts ← GUARDRAIL: a cancelled order must leave the seller Performance tab AND both admin revenue surfaces; a legacy order with no `storeSubtotals` must be skipped by all three rather than throw; also greps seller-performance.ts/admin-stats.ts for a stray `paymentStatus === 'paid'`, the drift that caused it
tests/reporting-invariants.test.ts ← INVARIANTS (properties, not scenarios): parts sum to whole, commission partitions revenue, day/month granularity agree, two surfaces produce one number, one business calendar incl. DST, and a buyer's multi-store order card totals the per-store figures printed inside it. Runs the per-order rules over fixtures
tests/admin-orders-page.test.ts ← the admin Orders tab's page (orders.ts#getAdminOrdersPage) asserted against filterAndSortOrders over the SAME rows, for every sort, filter and search — plus paging, the shared-timestamp tie-break and the "new since" boundary. Both sabotage cases verified red
tests/seller-orders-page-parity.test.ts ← the seller Orders tab's page (getSellerOrdersPage) against filterAndSortSellerOrders over the same rows — every sort both directions, the search, the cleared filter, paging — plus the watermark poll and the derived urgency groups. Found two disagreements: an order owned through its ITEMS with no slice row sorted first by amount in SQL (NULLs lead in DESC) and last in JS, and an uncapped search term
tests/toolbar-portal.test.ts    ← jsdom: one portal, two triggers — moving between them hands aria-expanded over, since that move never passes through close()
tests/product-search-normalize.test.ts ← the one search normalisation, pinned across its two implementations: migration 0006's SQL against normalizeHe, character for character over a corpus of traps, plus searchVisibleProducts against matchesQueryWords. Also the bounds — LIKE metacharacters are literal text, and the term is capped
tests/reporting-fuzz.test.ts    ← property-based fuzzing over the reporting layer: seeded PRNG (deterministic — a failure is reproducible from the seed in the label), hostile prices, 100% discounts, every payment×shipping combo, midnight/month-end/DST timestamps. Found the negative-revenue bug. Also asserts the reconciler DOES fire on a corrupted order — a check that never fires is indistinguishable from a broken one
tests/store-pageviews-db.test.ts ← store view counters against real Postgres: unique visitors counted once across a range (never summed from days), day/month buckets, the owner's own visits excluded upstream, many stores in one call
tests/product-pageviews-db.test.ts ← per-product view counters against real Postgres: every view counts, INCLUDING repeats by the same person (the deliberate difference from the store-level twin), and a malformed id is "no data", not a 500
tests/money-events-select.test.ts ← the money journal's vocabulary check (a valid type with no rows must come back EMPTY, never unfiltered) + selectMoneyEvents ordering/narrowing over the whole journal
tests/admin-tab-params.test.ts  ← scans /admin + its query parsers: every param read must be claimed by exactly one tab in ADMIN_TAB_PARAMS, every rendered tab must be registered, and stripForeignTabParams keeps only the active tab's own params
tests/admin-moneylog-filter.test.ts ← the money journal's search/window/permalink: which fields a term may match (incl. the Hebrew label + the 8-char order-id prefix the table shows), ANDed terms, the 200-char cap, the BUSINESS-day window, an inverted range swapping instead of returning nothing, and eventPage resolving a row to its page (null when absent)
tests/header-stability.test.ts ← GUARDRAIL: the one element on every page must not move or blink. No page hides `documentElement` (the store page did, to cover a scroll restore, and took the FIXED header with it for half a second on every return to a store); every page passes `storeMode`, which is not “this is a store” but the flag Header.astro reads to pick its ONE row mechanism; and only BaseLayout declares `id="main-content"` (seven pages nested a second one, duplicating the id the skip link targets).
tests/header-store-line.test.ts ← GUARDRAIL: the header draws ONE bottom line, the shared 1px hairline — no `::after`, no `--store-glow`, no glow host. Carries why the store-colour bar was removed after four attempts (its colour is sampled from the logo on the CLIENT, so it painted grey and changed a third of a second later, every store load) and what a future attempt would need: a colour the SERVER has at render time.
tests/busy-label-ellipsis.test.ts ← GUARDRAIL: a busy label never spells its own ellipsis — every one of them renders beside `.dot-pulse`, three animated dots, so “טוען...” put six dots on one button. Covers both the translation strings and a label hard-coded inline at a dot-pulse call site.
tests/instructions-integrity.test.ts ← does THIS file still describe the repo it lives in: every pointer in the always-read rules resolves, every `path ← note` in Project structure exists on disk, every tracked src/ file is documented. Replaces the manual end-of-session audit; runs in <0.5s. Cannot check whether a note is TRUE — only that it points somewhere real
tests/verify-doc-inputs.test.ts ← every `.md` a test READS must count towards verify.mjs's cache fingerprint. It excluded all markdown as "input to nothing", which stopped being true when the two entries below started asserting against THIS file — a docs-only edit then left `verify --all` reporting green from cache without running them (a real false green, 2026-08-04). Guards the assumption, not the one filename: a future test reading another doc fails here
tests/migration-ledger.test.ts  ← migration numbering has no duplicates or gaps and every file explains itself, plus the runner's ledger-vs-disk guard in both directions (and that it never deletes a stale row itself)
tests/instructions-budget.test.ts ← the always-read slice of THIS file, measured in characters and ratcheted: it may only shrink. The old line-count budget could not see a 4x growth because the lines are paragraphs. Failing it means relocate rationale into the file it governs — never delete it, and never raise the ceiling
tests/session-hooks.test.ts     ← the hooks that shape a session, because one that stops speaking up is silent by definition. one-way-to-verify.sh: a hand-run astro check / tsc / whole vitest is blocked, while a single test file, a -t filter and `npm run verify` itself go through (its risk is over-blocking, so most cases are things it must allow). money-rules-on-contact.sh: fires on money paths INCLUDING modules that don't exist yet, stays silent elsewhere, once per session, and names PostToolUse — PreToolUse has no additionalContext, so the same hook on the wrong event runs, emits valid JSON and is dropped
tests/money-guards.test.ts      ← the rules that keep working on code that doesn't exist yet: SCANS src/lib + src/pages/api + src/scripts for hand-rolled money rounding, a bare paid/cancelled status check, a re-listed status array, a calendar day from toISOString, an inlined timezone, and a money endpoint missing its idempotency key or journal write. Scan, not a file list — a new module is covered the moment it exists. Adding a file to an allowlist means you are creating a second definition of the rule, which is the bug
tests/order-status-rules.test.ts ← the status table cannot be half-filled: parses the Order type and fails if a status has no row, asserts every row answers every column, the whole 3×6 matrix exhaustively, and that no call site re-implements the rules
tests/checkout-idempotency.test.ts ← the double-charge guard: replay of a completed checkout, two submits racing, a failed attempt not locking the buyer out, an abandoned claim reclaimable after its TTL, a ledger write failure never undoing a real charge, key validated as untrusted input
tests/bot-detect.test.ts        ← GUARDRAIL: imports AI_AGENTS from the robots.txt route and asserts every crawler named there is filtered out of the view counters; also that real Chrome/iPhone/Android pass and the CUBOT phone brand isn't misread as a bot
tests/cross-origin-boundary.test.ts ← GUARDRAIL: the custom-domain→platform boundary — which paths are the platform's (and that the two lists account for every RESERVED_SLUGS entry), the signed identity token refuses anything it did not sign or that has expired, and a cart in a URL fragment is bounded, control-char-free and never throws
tests/robots-txt.test.ts        ← GUARDRAIL: robots.txt is host-dependent — a seller's domain names ITS OWN sitemap and never the platform's, /api/feed/ stays crawlable on the platform host only, and the platform host never costs a DB lookup
tests/image-url.test.ts         ← sanitizeImageUrl — scheme/shape rejection + the percent-encoding that defuses an attribute breakout
tests/html-escape.test.ts       ← escapeHtml behaviour + the sweep forbidding a new local escaper copy
tests/json-script.test.ts       ← jsonForScript behaviour + the sweep forbidding a bare JSON.stringify inside set:html
tests/seller-auth-password.test.ts ← password storage properties: scrypt not HMAC, legacy records still verify + upgrade on login, constant-time compare, no dev AUTH_SECRET fallback in prod
tests/calc-var-unit.test.ts   ← GUARDRAIL: a custom property consumed inside any calc() may never be declared unitless-zero — calc cannot add a bare number to a length, so every calc reading it dies silently at once (killed the dash-tabs edge fades, 2026-08-02); var(--x, 0px) fallbacks do NOT catch it, a fallback only fires for an undefined property
tests/db-connect.test.ts     ← GUARDRAIL: the Postgres TLS rule exists twice (app TS + ops .mjs) and this fails the moment they diverge, incl. that verification is on unless the connection string says sslmode=disable/no-verify
tests/db-import.test.ts       ← runs migrations/*.sql and the import on an in-process Postgres (PGlite — Postgres compiled to WASM, so constraints really fire). Covers the §9 checks, per-store slug uniqueness, duplicate payment_ref, the atomic variant-stock decrement that retired mutex.ts, and that no boolean column is nullable. Runs against tests/fixtures/db-data. It used to import the real data/*.json a second time too; those files were deleted in stage 3 and no coverage went with them, because data/ was gitignored and that half had never run on any push
tests/store-products-db.test.ts ← the catalog against a real Postgres: nested fields surviving the import (§7.14), absent flags staying visible (§7.12), agorot round-trip (§7.7), same slug in two stores (§7.1), and the stock decrement — including a combo that carries only a SKU selling from the shared pool. Written from scratch because the old coverage would have stayed green on a module that returned an empty catalog
tests/seed-db.test.ts         ← the seeders' DB half: a seeded store is reachable through getStoreBySlug/getVisibleProductsByStoreId, a failed write leaves the previous set intact, and purge cascades
tests/fixtures/db-data/       ← a committed dataset carrying the traps the real data taught us (§7.1 cross-store slug collision, §7.12 absent flags, §7.3 two bucket shapes, §7.7 binary tail, §7.11 case-only duplicate email, one orphan of every kind). Exists because data/*.json could never be committed — runtime state holding real buyer names — so without it CI verified the import by importing nothing. Since stage 3 deleted those files it is the ONLY dataset the import runs against, and tests/helpers/test-db.ts builds every test file's database from it
scripts/verify.mjs              ← `npm run verify`, the mid-session checkpoint: picks the checks the working diff actually needs, runs them concurrently off their own caches, prints only failures and always names what it skipped. `--all` forces everything (what the Stop hook runs). Also holds the two `astro check` false-positives and the `astro build` trick for locating a compile error
scripts/generate-brand-assets.mjs ← `npm run brand:assets`. Renders the lockup to PNG/JPEG through Playwright for the three surfaces that cannot take an SVG: the order-mail header, the og:image share card and the iOS home-screen icon. Draws the SAME paths as the site from src/lib/brand-lockup.ts (node imports the .ts directly), so it no longer re-types the typography and cannot leave a stale logo in someone's inbox. Run by hand; its output is committed
scripts/worktree-setup.mjs      ← `npm run worktree:setup`, the FIRST command inside a new worktree (Workflow §4). A checkout has no node_modules/.env, so verify would be red on the tooling before a check ran; this clones node_modules by APFS reference (~10s, ~0 disk), DROPS the cloned .cache so no other tree's answer can be read as this one's, copies .env + .claude/settings.local.json, then runs a full cold `verify --all` to prove the tree works and leave its caches warm. Its header holds the measurement of why two sessions in one tree deadlock
scripts/copy-review.mjs         ← `npm run copy:review [-- section ...]` → `.tmp-copy-review.txt` (gitignored), one stanza per key with the Hebrew alone on its own line and the English under it for reference; the user edits only the Hebrew; `npm run copy:apply` writes the changed strings back by exact source range and names every code line still carrying the OLD Hebrew as a `?? 'טקסט'` fallback; `set-en <file.json>` writes the matching English. Reviewing copy inside translations.ts is what this replaces — 1200 strings, two languages 1300 lines apart, RTL text inside Latin syntax. Its parser is a real scanner, not a per-line regex, because the block holds comments and an array of objects. **Apply REFUSES on the three ways hand-editing damages a string instead of rewording it** (all three happened on the first review, none visible in the result): a deleted `[key]` line — caught by the count the export declares, or every edit under it vanishes silently; the `en:` reference line joined into the Hebrew; a dropped `{placeholder}`; an emptied value. Stray/doubled spaces are tidied and always named
scripts/                        ← dev/ops one-offs, never imported by src: seed-demo-data.mjs (`npm run seed:demo`, demo catalog, `-- --clean` removes only demo rows) · seed-showcase-stores.mjs (`npm run seed:showcase`, the 4 platform showcase stores — a WRITER only since 2026-08-12; who they are and what they sell live in scripts/lib/showcase/) · generate-showcase-images.mjs (`npm run showcase:images`, Gemini → Cloudinary, idempotent by manifest) · export-showcase-prompts.mjs (`npm run showcase:prompts` → a CSV of every prompt; the by-hand route, rejected 2026-08-12 — kept only because a CSV of the prompts is a useful thing to read) · setup-claude-memory.sh (new-machine memory restore). Both need DATABASE_URL and write to Postgres via scripts/lib/seed-db.mjs, whose header holds the purge gate: a seeder NAMES a scope ('demo'/'showcase') and never writes a WHERE, and a scope that is not a subset of the disposable set is refused before any DELETE (tests/seed-purge-gate.test.ts)
migrations/0001_init.sql        ← the WHOLE schema in one file: tables, indexes, constraints (DB_MIGRATION_PLAN.md §4/§6/§7). Every optional boolean is NOT NULL DEFAULT false (§7.12) and product slug uniqueness is per-store (§7.1) — both measured against the real data, both invisible by reading
migrations/0002_category_sibling_name.sql ← UNIQUE (store_id, COALESCE(parent_id, zero-uuid), name): a duplicate sibling category name is refused by an index, not by a read before the write. The COALESCE is why it also covers the TOP level, where two NULL parents are never equal in SQL
migrations/0003_variant_stock_optional.sql ← product_variant_stock.stock becomes NULLable: `variantStock` is a PARTIAL map, so a combo carrying only a SKU means "no override, sell from the shared pool" — storing 0 there reads as "sold out" and silently takes the combo off the shelf
migrations/0016_campaign_paused_reason_no_image.sql ← widens ad_campaigns.paused_reason's CHECK for 'no-image'. A product with no photo is not in the Merchant/Catalog feed at all, so a boost over such products advertised nothing while reading "פעיל". Could not reuse either existing value: they differ in WHO undoes the pause, and this one is self-healing like 'out-of-stock' but must not be LABELLED it — "אזל מהמלאי" on a card about a photo is a false statement to the seller. Widening a CHECK is additive; the migration ships before the code that writes the value
migrations/0012_store_image_source.sql ← stores.banner_image_source / profile_image_source: the UNCROPPED upload behind each store image, so re-framing cuts from the original instead of from the previous crop (panning a square crop inside a square viewport moves nothing). Read only by the dashboard widget; every public surface still reads the cropped column, which is why it is a second column and not a `c_crop` baked into the URL — cdn.ts would treat that as "already optimized" and stop resizing it
migrations/0008_product_brand.sql ← store_products.brand: the manufacturer's brand for a RESELLER. NULL keeps the store-name fallback the feed and JSON-LD always used, so no backfill — additive, and the previous deploy neither reads nor writes it
migrations/0006_product_search_text.sql ← store_products.search_text, a GENERATED STORED column + gin_trgm index (expression replaced by 0027). Its expression is `product_search_text()`, a character-for-character port of product-listing.ts#normalizeHe (nikud, the five final letters, geresh, punctuation, whitespace) — a FUNCTION and not an inline expression because a generated column must be IMMUTABLE and array_to_string is not marked so. The fold is what makes a search for "טלפון" find "ספר טלפונים"; a raw ILIKE silently stops matching exactly the Hebrew cases the normaliser exists for
migrations/0027_search_text_variants.sql ← search_text now also carries the product's VARIANT VALUES, so a shopper finds a shirt by "צהוב" when yellow exists only as a variant option. Which values qualify (a letter, 2+ chars — `XL` in, `S` and `42` out) and why the test is on the VALUE and not the dimension name: src/lib/product-search-text.ts. Drop+re-add of the generated column, one transaction — a generation expression cannot be altered in place
docker-compose.yml              ← local Postgres for development (pgvector/pgvector:pg18, TZ=UTC — the major version TRACKS Neon, which moved 17→18 under us; measured 18.4 on 2026-08-09 from the backup workflow's first run, not assumed). `docker compose up -d db` → `npm run db:migrate`. Production is Neon/eu-central-1
scripts/db-migrate.mjs          ← `npm run db:migrate`: applies migrations/*.sql in filename order, one transaction each, recorded with a checksum so editing an applied migration is an error rather than a silent no-op. `-- --dry` lists without applying; `-- --check` is the same list but EXITS 1, which is what `npm run verify` runs whenever migrations/ changed — the suite builds its OWN database from these files, so a migration written and never applied stays invisible to every other check while the running app throws `column X does not exist` (shipped a broken dev site 2026-08-04). No DATABASE_URL = reported skipped, not failed **It compares the ledger against disk in BOTH directions:** a file the ledger doesn't know runs; a ROW naming a file that isn't there is reported and exits non-zero, because a database whose history matches no checkout cannot be shown to reach the same schema as a fresh environment. Never auto-deleted — only a person can tell a renumbering to forget from a migration file somebody lost (`tests/migration-ledger.test.ts`; hit for real on 2026-08-05 by two sessions both writing `0010_*`).
scripts/backup-db.mjs           ← `npm run backup:db`, and normally NOT run by hand: the daily off-site backup, driven by .github/workflows/backup.yml. pg_dump (plain SQL, --no-owner --no-acl, legible by any Postgres) -> gzip -> Cloudflare R2, then READS THE OBJECT BACK and compares a SHA-256 — a 200 on the write proves only that the request was accepted. Two refusals that matter more than the happy path: a dump under 20KB is not uploaded (pg_dump can exit 0 having written only a header), and pg_dump is spawned from an ABSOLUTE path (PG_DUMP_PATH), never resolved on PATH. WHY it exists: Neon Free keeps 6 hours of PITR and that history dies with the account
scripts/lib/s3-put.mjs          ← putObject/getObject/signRequest/sha256 — AWS SigV4 by hand for one PUT and one GET, no @aws-sdk dependency. Its header carries the two verification traps MEASURED against the real bucket: `content-length` is null through Node fetch (a size check reports 0 on a complete object) and R2 returns weak `W/"…"` and strong ETags for objects written in the same run — which is why the check is a read-back hash and not either of them. tests/s3-put.test.ts pins the canonical request against AWS's published vector
scripts/lib/pg-connect.mjs      ← createClient/requireDatabaseUrl/sslSetting for the ops scripts. Deliberate second copy of src/lib/db.ts's TLS rule (a .mjs script cannot import .ts); tests/db-connect.test.ts fails if the two disagree. Default VERIFIES the certificate — `rejectUnauthorized:false` accepts any cert, i.e. encrypted but unauthenticated
scripts/lib/db-import.mjs       ← the import logic (importAll), separate so tests/db-import.test.ts can drive it against an in-process Postgres. Owns the ILS→agorot conversion and the §7.3 shape normalisation
scripts/lib/seed-db.mjs         ← the DB half of both seeders: openSeedClient/purge/writeCatalog. Purge + write are ONE transaction, because both seeders fetch photos from the network first and a purge that committed alone would delete the showcase stores and put nothing back. tests/seed-db.test.ts asserts a seeded store is visible THROUGH the app's own readers — the assertion whose absence let both scripts report success over an empty catalog for three modules
scripts/lib/verify-import.mjs   ← the §9 anchors: row counts, money totals, visible-product count (§7.12), nested-field counts (§7.14), bucket counter sums (§7.15), buyer state (cart/wishlist/favourites) — one number from the JSON vs the same number from the DB. An anchor resolves its expected side from the JSON, never by subtracting the import's own "rows I skipped" report: that made the wishlist anchor restate the import instead of testing it, and it passed against an empty table
```

---

## Workflow
1. Read this file + `CURRENT_TASK.md`.
2. Do only what is in `CURRENT_TASK.md → Your instruction`.
3. Before reporting any change done: **`npm run verify -- --all` green** (add or extend a test first if the change touched money/auth/inventory/cart/checkout — see Hard rules → Testing), and — if it touched that same surface — a manual diff review against the known classes, since `/code-review` is user-triggered only (see Hard rules → Security review gate). Then mark a `✅ בוצע (date) — ...` line right under the specific numbered item in `CURRENT_TASK.md` it belongs to (not just at end-of-session) — this is how the user tracks what's done across parallel sessions, so never skip it once a numbered item is finished.
3.5. **Audit the AREA you worked in, not only your diff — one row per session (user, 2026-08-06).** A review reads what CHANGED; code untouched since it shipped is in no diff and never re-read, which is where the feed bugs lived. Areas + what counts as audited: the **Area audit** table in the `review-diff` skill. **A ✅ EXPIRES: `npm run audit:drift`** names the rows whose code moved since. Side-work, not the main task.

4. **Parallel sessions — a worktree each, always (2026-08-04; the cost was questioned and re-measured 2026-08-09, and the answer held — `one-session-per-tree.sh` carries the numbers).** Two in one tree deadlock on the tree-wide fingerprint every gate uses, so the turn never ends. Both that and the orphaned-branch risk it trades for are hook-enforced, reasoning in the hooks: `one-session-per-tree.sh`, `worktrees-outstanding.sh` (start), `worktree-handoff.sh` (Stop — a session is told what IT leaves behind, since the next session's start is too late). From a **fresh** session: `EnterWorktree`, then `npm run worktree:setup` FIRST — a checkout has no `node_modules`/`.env`, so verify is red before a check runs. One task per worktree, and **closing it out is MY call, not a question (2026-08-05)**: done + `verify -- --all` green → merge main in, ff main, `git log main..<branch>` empty, remove. Never push; never a LIVE or ORPHAN tree. `CURRENT_TASK.md` is shared/live — never write to it while the user edits it in their IDE.
5. **End of session** (trigger: "next session" / "we're done" / "end of session" / "סגור את הסשן") — do ALL of:
   - Update **Features built**: merge new items into existing bullets (never add a bullet if the feature already exists). One line per feature max. No implementation details — names + key gotchas only.
   - Update **Project structure**: add new files, remove deleted ones. One line per file.
   - Keep this file lean: merge bullets, drop details already recoverable from the code. Don't force-cut real, non-recoverable context (a decision, a gotcha) just to hit a number. **The budget on the ALWAYS-READ part is a RATCHET in CHARACTERS, enforced by `tests/instructions-budget.test.ts`** — the ceiling may only ever come down, and raising it is the same silencing move as regenerating the lint baseline. Adding a rule is fine — pay for it by **moving** an equivalent amount of rationale into the file it governs (a module header, a hook, a `GO_LIVE`/`DB_MIGRATION` row) and leaving the rule plus a pointer here. Relocation, never deletion: nothing this project learned may be lost to save bytes, and a gotcha read at the moment it applies beats one skimmed at session start. This budget does not apply to the whole file: the two reference sections are grepped, never read linearly, and the header protocol says explicitly not to delete them to save tokens. Measuring it has a trap, which that test carries. **Re-measure before acting on this** — quote the number, don't trust the one written here.
   - **Verifying this file against the repo is now automatic — `tests/instructions-integrity.test.ts`, inside `npm test` (2026-07-31).** It fails if a pointer in the always-read rules leads nowhere, if a `path ← note` line in **Project structure** names something that isn't on disk, or if a tracked `src/` file appears in neither an entry nor a directory group line. It runs in under half a second, so this stopped being an end-of-session chore you could skip. **What it cannot check is whether a note still tells the TRUTH** — both errors found the day it was written were a correct path with a false claim beside it. So when you touch a module, re-read its structure line, and confirm the truth in the CODE before rewriting one — never trust the prose you are correcting.
   - **Mark the area-audit row** (step 3.5) — or name the one still open. Duplicated here on purpose: a rule that fires only mid-session is one I drift past by the end.
   - **Never touch `CURRENT_TASK.md`'s `Your instruction` or `Next`** — only the user writes/edits/deletes those. There is no "Recommended next step" section anymore (discontinued 2026-07-16) — don't re-add one.
